IV.2.3
A useful integral identity

Before evaluating
F^{ij}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})
, we introduce the following integral, which will be used repeatedly below. It is defined by

I(\omega_{1},\omega_{2},\omega_{3},E_{1},E_{2},E_{3})\equiv\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\int_{\tau_{3}}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\int_{\tau_{2}}^{\beta}d\tau_{1}\;e^{-\tau_{3}(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})+\tau_{2}(E_{2}+i\omega_{2})-\tau_{1}(E_{1}+i\omega_{1})}

(S163)

A straightforward evaluation gives

\displaystyle I(\omega_{1},\omega_{2},\omega_{3},E_{1},E_{2},E_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\int_{\tau_{3}}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\;e^{-\tau_{3}(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})+\tau_{2}(E_{2}+i\omega_{2})}\frac{e^{-\tau_{2}(E_{1}+i\omega_{1})}-e^{-\beta(E_{1}+i\omega_{1})}}{E_{1}+i\omega_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E_{1}+i\omega_{1})}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\;e^{-\tau_{3}(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})}\bigg[\frac{e^{\beta(E_{2}-E_{1}+i\omega_{2}-i\omega_{1})}-e^{\tau_{3}(E_{2}-E_{1}+i\omega_{2}-i\omega_{1})}}{E_{2}-E_{1}+i\omega_{2}-i\omega_{1}}(1-\delta_{E_{1}E_{2}}\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}})+(\beta-\tau_{3})\delta_{E_{1}E_{2}}\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}

\displaystyle+e^{-\beta E_{1}}\frac{e^{\beta(E_{2}+i\omega_{2})}-e^{\tau_{3}(E_{2}+i\omega_{2})}}{E_{2}+i\omega_{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E_{1}+i\omega_{1})}\bigg\{\frac{1-\delta_{E_{1}E_{2}}\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}}{E_{2}-E_{1}+i\omega_{2}-i\omega_{1}}\bigg[e^{\beta(E_{2}-E_{1})}\frac{1-e^{-\beta(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})}}{E_{3}+i\omega_{3}}-\frac{1-e^{-\beta(E_{3}-E_{2}+E_{1}+i\omega_{3}-i\omega_{2}+i\omega_{1})}}{E_{3}-E_{2}+E_{1}+i\omega_{3}-i\omega_{2}+i\omega_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\delta_{E_{1}E_{2}}\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}\frac{-1+e^{-\beta(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})}+\beta(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})}{(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{-\beta E_{1}}}{E_{2}+i\omega_{2}}\bigg[-e^{\beta E_{2}}\frac{1-e^{-\beta(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})}}{E_{3}+i\omega_{3}}-\frac{1-e^{-\beta(E_{3}-E_{2}+i\omega_{3}-i\omega_{2})}}{E_{3}-E_{2}+i\omega_{3}-i\omega_{2}}(1-\delta_{E_{2}E_{3}}\delta_{\omega_{2}\omega_{3}})-\beta\delta_{E_{2}E_{3}}\delta_{\omega_{2}\omega_{3}}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E_{1}+i\omega_{1})}\bigg\{\frac{1-\delta_{E_{1}E_{2}}\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}}{E_{2}-E_{1}+i\omega_{2}-i\omega_{1}}\bigg[e^{\beta(E_{2}-E_{1})}\frac{1+e^{-\beta E_{3}}}{E_{3}+i\omega_{3}}-\frac{1+e^{-\beta(E_{3}-E_{2}+E_{1})}}{E_{3}-E_{2}+E_{1}+i\omega_{3}-i\omega_{2}+i\omega_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\delta_{E_{1}E_{2}}\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}\frac{-1-e^{-\beta E_{3}}+\beta(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})}{(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{-\beta E_{1}}}{E_{2}+i\omega_{2}}\bigg[-e^{\beta E_{2}}\frac{1+e^{-\beta E_{3}}}{E_{3}+i\omega_{3}}-\frac{1-e^{-\beta(E_{3}-E_{2})}}{E_{3}-E_{2}+i\omega_{3}-i\omega_{2}}(1-\delta_{E_{2}E_{3}}\delta_{\omega_{2}\omega_{3}})-\beta\delta_{E_{2}E_{3}}\delta_{\omega_{2}\omega_{3}}\bigg]\bigg\}

After some algebra, this expression can be rewritten as

\displaystyle I(\omega_{1},\omega_{2},\omega_{3},E_{1},E_{2},E_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E_{1}+i\omega_{1})}\bigg\{\frac{1-\delta_{E_{1}E_{2}}\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}}{E_{2}-E_{1}+i\omega_{2}-i\omega_{1}}\bigg[\frac{e^{-\beta(E_{1}-E_{2})}(1+e^{-\beta E_{3}})}{{E_{3}}+i\omega_{3}}-\frac{1+e^{-\beta(E_{3}-E_{2}+E_{1})}}{E_{3}-E_{2}+E_{1}+i\omega_{3}-i\omega_{2}+i\omega_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\delta_{E_{1}E_{2}}\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}\frac{1+e^{-\beta E_{3}}}{(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})^{2}}-\frac{e^{-\beta(E_{1}-E_{2})}(1+e^{-\beta E_{3}})}{(E_{2}+i\omega_{2})(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})}-\frac{e^{-\beta E_{1}}[1-e^{-\beta(E_{3}-E_{2})}]}{(E_{2}+i\omega_{2})(E_{3}-E_{2}+i\omega_{3}-i\omega_{2})}(1-\delta_{E_{2}E_{3}}\delta_{\omega_{2}\omega_{3}})\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta_{E_{1}E_{2}}\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}}{(E_{1}+i\omega_{1})(E_{3}+i\omega_{3})}-\frac{e^{-\beta E_{1}}\delta_{E_{2}E_{3}}\delta_{\omega_{2}\omega_{3}}}{(E_{1}+i\omega_{1})(E_{2}+i\omega_{2})}

(S164)

IV.2.4
Evaluation of
\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle_{\gamma=0}
for
i=j

We now evaluate
section

IV.2
for the case of
i=j

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle_{\gamma=0}

(S165)

where we take
\tau_{1},\tau_{2},\tau_{3}\in(0,\beta]
.
A nonzero contribution arises only when, after time ordering, the two creation operators are not adjacent. Equivalently, the two annihilation operators must also not be adjacent.
Therefore, nonzero contributions arise only in the following two time-ordering sectors:

\tau_{1}>\tau_{2}>\tau_{3},\;\tau_{3}>\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}

(S166)

•

For
\tau_{1}>\tau_{2}>\tau_{3}
, using
eq.

S150
we have

\displaystyle\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-(\beta-\tau_{1})E_{n}-(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})E_{m}-(\tau_{2}-\tau_{3})E_{n}-\tau_{3}E_{m}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-\beta E_{n}+\tau_{1}(E_{n}-E_{m})+\tau_{2}(E_{m}-E_{n})+\tau_{3}(E_{n}-E_{m})}

(S167)

Here,
|n\rangle
denotes the eigenstate of the atomic Hamiltonian
H_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}
(
eq.

S114
) with energy
E_{n}
.
Moreover, since the flavor occupation operator commutes with the atomic Hamiltonian,
[f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i},H_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}]=0
, we chose the energy eigenstates
\ket{n}
to be simultaneous eigenstates of the flavor number operators
f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}
.
We next evaluate the product of matrix elements
\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle{m}|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle
.
This product is nonzero only when
\ket{m}=e^{i\phi}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}
.
Since the overall phase is irrelevant, we choose
\phi=0
for simplicity.
Then the matrix element becomes

\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle{m}|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle=(\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n})^{4}=\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

(S168)

After Fourier transforming (
section

IV.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\int_{\tau_{3}}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\int_{\tau_{2}}^{\beta}d\tau_{1}\;e^{-\beta E_{n}+\tau_{1}(E_{n}-E_{m})+\tau_{2}(E_{m}-E_{n})+\tau_{3}(E_{n}-E_{m})}e^{-i\omega\tau_{3}+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-\beta E_{n}}I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n})

(S169)

•

For
\tau_{3}>\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}
, we similarly obtain

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-(\beta-\tau_{3})E_{n}-(\tau_{3}-\tau_{2})E_{m}-(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})E_{n}-\tau_{1}E_{m}}

(S170)

Again taking
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}
, the product of matrix elements becomes

\displaystyle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle=(\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n})^{4}=\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

(S171)

Thus, the Fourier transformation is

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{1}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\int_{\tau_{2}}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\;e^{-\beta E_{n}-\tau_{1}(E_{m}-E_{n})+\tau_{2}(E_{m}-E_{n})-\tau_{3}(E_{m}-E_{n})-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}-i\omega\tau_{3}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-\beta E_{n}}I(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n})

(S172)

Summing
eqs.

S169
and
S172
and using
eq.

S223
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\int_{\tau_{3}}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\int_{\tau_{2}}^{\beta}d\tau_{1}\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle e^{-i\omega\tau_{3}+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}[I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E,E,E)-I(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},E,E,E)]\bigg|_{E=E_{m}-E_{n}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}})[1+e^{-\beta(E_{m}-E_{n})}]}{(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega^{\prime})(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega)}

(S173)

In the zero-temperature limit, and using
eq.

S121
, the previous equation becomes

\displaystyle\lim_{\beta\to+\infty}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}})[1+e^{-\beta(E_{m}-E_{n})}]}{(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega^{\prime})(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}^{-1}\lim_{\beta\to+\infty}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}})[1+e^{-\beta(E_{m}-E_{n})}]}{(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega^{\prime})(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}^{-1}\lim_{\beta\to+\infty}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}})[e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}+e^{-\beta(E_{m}-E_{gs})}]}{(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega^{\prime})(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\in GS\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}})}{(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega^{\prime})(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega)}

\displaystyle+{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\;\;m\in GS\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}})}{(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega^{\prime})(E_{m}-E_{n}+i\omega)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\in GS\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}})}{(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega)}

\displaystyle+{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\;\;m\in GS\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}})}{(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega^{\prime})(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega)}

(S174)

The first sum,
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\in GS\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}
, runs over all ground states
\ket{n}
in which flavor
i
is occupied; the number of such ground states is
\binom{N_{f}-1}{n_{f}-1}
.
The second sum,
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\;\;m\in GS\end{subarray}}
, runs over all ground states
\ket{m}
in which flavor
i
is unoccupied; the number of such states is
\binom{N_{f}-1}{n_{f}}
.
Putting everything together, we find that in the zero-temperature limit the Fourier transform of the four-point correlation function for
i=j
is

\displaystyle I_{ii}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\int_{\tau_{3}}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\int_{\tau_{2}}^{\beta}d\tau_{1}\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle e^{-i\omega\tau_{3}+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}[I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E,E,E)-I(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},E,E,E)]\bigg|_{E=E_{m}-E_{n}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\frac{N_{f}-n_{f}}{N_{f}}\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}

(S175)

Finally, combining
eqs.

S119
,
IV.2
and
S175
, we obtain

\displaystyle F^{ii}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2},\tau_{3}}\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle_{\gamma=0}e^{-i\omega\tau_{3}+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}}+G_{f,loc}(i\omega)G_{f,loc}(i\omega^{\prime})-{\delta_{i\omega,i\omega^{\prime}}G_{f,loc}(i\omega)G_{f,loc}(i\omega^{\prime})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1+\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})\bigg[\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}\frac{1}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}+\frac{N_{f}-n_{f}}{N_{f}}\frac{1}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N_{f}^{2}}\bigg(\frac{n_{f}}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{N_{f}-n_{f}}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{n_{f}}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{N_{f}-n_{f}}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{f}^{2}}\frac{(-1+\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{-1})}\bigg\{n_{f}N_{f}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})+(N_{f}-n_{f})N_{f}(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})

\displaystyle-[n_{f}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})+(N_{f}-n_{f})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})][n_{f}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})+(N_{f}-n_{f})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})]\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{f}^{2}}\frac{(-1+\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})

\displaystyle+(N_{f}-n_{f})n_{f}(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})-n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})-(N_{f}-n_{f})n_{f}(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}\frac{(-1+\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})+(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})-(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})-(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}}\frac{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}(-1+\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})

(S176)

In summary, we obtain

\displaystyle F^{ii}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})=

\displaystyle-I_{ii}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})+G_{f,loc}(i\omega)G_{f,loc}(i\omega^{\prime})-{\delta_{i\omega,i\omega^{\prime}}G_{f,loc}(i\omega)G_{f,loc}(i\omega^{\prime})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}}\frac{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}(-1+\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})

(S177)

IV.2.5
Evaluation of
\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle_{\gamma=0}
for
i\neq j

We next consider the case
i\neq j
:

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle_{\gamma=0}

(S178)

There are six possible time-ordering sectors:

\displaystyle\tau_{1}>\tau_{2}>\tau_{3}

\displaystyle\tau_{1}>\tau_{3}>\tau_{2}

\displaystyle\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}>\tau_{3}

\displaystyle\tau_{2}>\tau_{3}>\tau_{1}

\displaystyle\tau_{3}>\tau_{1}>\tau_{2}

\displaystyle\tau_{3}>\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}

(S179)

•

For
\tau_{1}>\tau_{2}>\tau_{3}
, we find

\displaystyle\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-(\beta-\tau_{1})E_{n}-(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})E_{m}-(\tau_{2}-\tau_{3})E_{n}-\tau_{3}E_{s}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-\beta E_{n}+\tau_{1}(E_{n}-E_{m})+\tau_{2}(E_{m}-E_{n})+\tau_{3}(E_{n}-E_{s})}

(S180)

Denote
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}
,
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}
, the matrix element is evaluated as

\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle=(\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n})^{2}(\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n})^{2}=\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

(S181)

After Fourier transforming, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\int_{\tau_{3}}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\int_{\tau_{2}}^{\beta}d\tau_{1}\;e^{-\beta E_{n}+\tau_{1}(E_{n}-E_{m})+\tau_{2}(E_{m}-E_{n})+\tau_{3}(E_{n}-E_{s})}e^{-i\omega\tau_{3}+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-\beta E_{n}}I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n})}

(S182)

In the last line we used
E_{s}=E_{m}
as the two states contain the same number of
f
electrons.

Using
eq.

S221
, the integral evaluates to

\displaystyle I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E=E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}\bigg[-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{E+i\omega}+\frac{1}{E+i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)+1-e^{-\beta E}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

(S183)

Taking the zero-temperature limit
\beta\to\infty
, we obtain

\displaystyle e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E=E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n})\bigg|_{\beta\rightarrow\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}\bigg[-\frac{\delta_{n,gs}+\delta_{m,gs}}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{E+i\omega}+\frac{1}{E+i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)+\delta_{n,gs}-\delta_{m,gs}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

(S184)

and the Fourier transform becomes

\displaystyle{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n})\bigg|_{\beta\to\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\bigg\{\sum_{n\in GS}\frac{\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}}{(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega)}\bigg[-\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)+1\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
m\in GS\end{subarray}}\frac{\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg[-\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega)}\bigg[-\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)+1\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg[-\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

(S185)

In previous equation,
\sum_{n\in GS}
counts the ground states in which both flavors
i
and
j
are occupied, while
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
m\in GS\end{subarray}}
counts the corresponding charge
+1
excitation states. Their multiplicities are
\binom{N_{f}-2}{n_{f}-2}
and
\binom{N_{f}-2}{n_{f}-1}
, respectively.

•

For
\tau_{1}>\tau_{3}>\tau_{2}
, we find

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m,u}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|u\rangle\langle u|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-(\beta-\tau_{1})E_{n}-(\tau_{1}-\tau_{3})E_{m}-(\tau_{3}-\tau_{2})E_{u}-\tau_{2}E_{s}}

(S186)

Denote
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}
,
\ket{u}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}
and
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}
.
For
i\neq j
, the matrix element is evaluated as

\displaystyle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|u\rangle\langle u|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}(\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n})^{2}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n})^{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

(S187)

where we have used the fact
\ket{n}
is also the eigenstate of the flavor number operators
f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}
for all
i
, so that
\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}=\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}
.
The Fourier transform gives

\displaystyle(-1)\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m,u}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|u\rangle\langle u|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\int_{\tau_{2}}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\int_{\tau_{3}}^{\beta}d\tau_{1}\;e^{-\beta E_{n}-\tau_{2}(E_{s}-E_{u})+\tau_{3}(E_{m}-E_{u})-\tau_{1}(E_{m}-E_{n})+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}-i\omega\tau_{3}-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m,u}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|u\rangle\langle u|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-\beta E_{n}}I(\omega^{\prime},-\omega,-\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{u},E_{s}-E_{u})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{u}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(\omega^{\prime},-\omega,-\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{u},E_{s}-E_{u})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{u}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(\omega^{\prime},-\omega,-\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{u},E_{m}-E_{u})}

(S188)

In the last line, we used
E_{s}=E_{m}
as the two states contain the same number of
f
electrons.

Using
eq.

S164
, the integral evaluates to

\displaystyle I(\omega^{\prime},-\omega,-\omega^{\prime},E=E_{m}-E_{n},E^{\prime}=E_{m}-E_{u},E_{m}-E_{u})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg\{\frac{1-\delta_{EE^{\prime}}\delta_{\omega^{\prime},-\omega}}{E^{\prime}-E-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{e^{-\beta(E-E^{\prime})}(1+e^{-\beta E^{\prime}})}{E^{\prime}-i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{E+i\omega}\bigg]-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E^{\prime}}}{(E^{\prime}-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{\delta_{EE^{\prime}}\delta_{\omega^{\prime},-\omega}}{(E^{\prime}-i\omega^{\prime})}+\frac{e^{-\beta(E-E^{\prime})}}{(E^{\prime}-i\omega)}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta_{EE^{\prime}}\delta_{\omega^{\prime},-\omega}-e^{-\beta E}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}-i\omega^{\prime})}

(S189)

Taking the zero-temperature limit, we obtain

\displaystyle e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(\omega^{\prime},-\omega,-\omega^{\prime},E=E_{m}-E_{n},E^{\prime}=E_{m}-E_{u},E_{m}-E_{u})\bigg|_{\beta\rightarrow\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg\{\frac{1-\delta_{E_{n}E_{u}}\delta_{\omega^{\prime},-\omega}}{E^{\prime}-E-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\delta_{u,gs}+\delta_{m,gs}}{E^{\prime}-i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{\delta_{n,gs}+\delta_{m,gs}}{E+i\omega}\bigg]-

\displaystyle-\frac{\delta_{n,gs}+\delta_{m,gs}}{(E^{\prime}-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{\delta_{E_{n}E_{u}}\delta_{\omega^{\prime},-\omega}}{(E^{\prime}-i\omega^{\prime})}-\frac{\delta_{u,gs}+\delta_{m,gs}}{(E^{\prime}-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}-i\omega)}\bigg\}+\frac{\delta_{E_{n}E_{u}}\delta_{\omega^{\prime},-\omega}\delta_{n,gs}-\delta_{m,gs}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}-i\omega^{\prime})}

and the Fourier transform becomes

\displaystyle{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{u}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(\omega^{\prime},-\omega,-\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{u},E_{m}-E_{u})\bigg|_{\beta\to\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{u}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})}\frac{1-\delta_{\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}}\delta_{\omega^{\prime},-\omega}}{\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{-1}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{m,gs}}{-\Delta_{-1}-i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{\delta_{m,gs}}{-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega}\bigg)-\frac{1}{\beta(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{\delta_{m,gs}\delta_{\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}}\delta_{\omega^{\prime},-\omega}}{(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})}\frac{\delta_{m,gs}}{(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega)}+\frac{\delta_{m,gs}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{+2}+i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}\frac{\delta_{u,gs}}{\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{+2}+i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{\delta_{u,gs}}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega)}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{1}{\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}}\frac{\delta_{n,gs}}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})n_{f}}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}-\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{+2}+i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{1}{\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})n_{f}}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}-\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega)}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{1}{\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}

(S191)

where
\Delta_{\pm 2}=E(n_{f}\pm 2)-E(n_{f})
are the energies of charge
\pm 2
excitations respectively.
Besides, we also use that
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
u\in GS\end{subarray}}
counts the charge
+2
excitation states in which both
i,j
flavors are occupied. And the multiplicity is
\binom{N_{f}-2}{n_{f}}

•

For
\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}>\tau_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-(\beta-\tau_{2})E_{n}-(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})E_{m}-(\tau_{1}-\tau_{3})E_{n}-\tau_{3}E_{s}}

(S192)

Denote
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}
and
\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}
, then the matrix element is evaluated as

\displaystyle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle=(\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n})^{2}(\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n})^{2}=\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

(S193)

And the Fourier transform is

\displaystyle(-1)\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\int_{\tau_{3}}^{\beta}d\tau_{1}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\;e^{-\beta E_{n}-\tau_{3}(E_{s}-E_{n})+\tau_{1}(E_{m}-E_{n})-\tau_{2}(E_{m}-E_{n})-i\omega\tau_{3}+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|n\rangle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-\beta E_{n}}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}(1-\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})}

(S194)

Using
eq.

S164
, the integral is

\displaystyle I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega^{\prime},\omega,E=E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E^{\prime}=E_{s}-E_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg\{-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E^{\prime}}}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}\bigg[\frac{1}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}+\frac{1}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg]-\frac{e^{-\beta E}-e^{-\beta E^{\prime}}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}-E+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}(1-\delta_{EE^{\prime}}\delta_{-\omega^{\prime}\omega})\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1-e^{-\beta E}\delta_{EE^{\prime}}\delta_{-\omega^{\prime}\omega}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}

Taking zero-temperature limit, we obtain

\displaystyle e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega^{\prime},\omega,E=E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E^{\prime}=E_{s}-E_{n})\bigg|_{\beta\rightarrow\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg\{-\frac{\delta_{n,gs}+\delta_{s,gs}}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}\bigg[\frac{1}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}+\frac{1}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg]-\frac{\delta_{m,gs}-\delta_{s,gs}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}-E+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}(1-\delta_{E_{s}E_{m}}\delta_{-\omega^{\prime}\omega})\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta_{n,gs}-\delta_{m,gs}\delta_{E_{s},E_{m}}\delta_{-\omega^{\prime}\omega}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}

(S196)

and the Fourier transform becomes

\displaystyle-{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}(1-\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})\bigg|_{\beta\to\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}(1-\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

\displaystyle\bigg\{\frac{1}{\beta(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{\delta_{n,gs}}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}\bigg[\frac{1}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}+\frac{1}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg]-\frac{\delta_{n,gs}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{\delta_{s,gs}}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}\bigg[\frac{1}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}+\frac{1}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg]+\frac{1}{\beta(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{-\delta_{s,gs}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}-E+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}(1-\delta_{E_{s}E_{m}}\delta_{-\omega^{\prime}\omega})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{\delta_{m,gs}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}-E+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}(1-\delta_{E_{s}E_{m}}\delta_{-\omega^{\prime}\omega})+\frac{\delta_{m,gs}\delta_{E_{s},E_{m}}\delta_{-\omega^{\prime}\omega}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega)}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)-1\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{\Delta_{+1}-i\omega}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(-\Delta_{-1}-i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}\frac{1}{(\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}

(S197)

In this case,
\sum_{n\in GS}
,
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
s\in GS\end{subarray}}
,
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
m\in GS\end{subarray}}
count the ground state, charge
+1
excitation and charge
-1
excitation states
\ket{n}
that contain the
i
-the flavor but not contain the
j
-th flavor. The multiplicities of them are
\binom{N_{f}-2}{n_{f}-1}
,
\binom{N_{f}-2}{n_{f}}
and
\binom{N_{f}-2}{n_{f}-2}
, respectively.

For
\tau_{2}>\tau_{3}>\tau_{1}
, we find

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m,u}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|u\rangle\langle u|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-(\beta-\tau_{2})E_{n}-(\tau_{2}-\tau_{3})E_{m}-(\tau_{3}-\tau_{1})E_{u}-\tau_{1}E_{s}}

(S198)

Denote
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}
,
\ket{u}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{s}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}
and
\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}
, then the matrix element is evaluated as

\displaystyle\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|u\rangle\langle u|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}(\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n})^{2}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}

(S199)

And the Fourier transform is

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m,u}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|u\rangle\langle u|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau_{1}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\beta}d\tau_{3}\int_{\tau_{3}}^{\beta}d\tau_{2}\;e^{-\beta E_{n}-\tau_{1}(E_{s}-E_{u})+\tau_{3}(E_{m}-E_{u})-\tau_{2}(E_{m}-E_{n})-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}-i\omega\tau_{3}+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,s,m,u}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}|u\rangle\langle u|f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}|s\rangle\langle s|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}|n\rangle e^{-\beta E_{n}}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega,\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{u},E_{s}-E_{u})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{u}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega,\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{u},E_{s}-E_{u})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{u}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n},\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}(1-\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega,\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})}

(S200)

Using
eq.

S164
, the integral is

\displaystyle I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega,\omega^{\prime},E=E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E^{\prime}=E_{s}-E_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg\{-\frac{(1+e^{-\beta E^{\prime}})}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega^{\prime}\omega})}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega)}+\frac{\delta_{\omega^{\prime}\omega}}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega^{\prime})}+\frac{1}{(E-i\omega)}\bigg]-\frac{e^{-\beta E}-e^{-\beta E^{\prime}}}{(E-i\omega)(E^{\prime}-E+i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega)}(1-\delta_{EE^{\prime}}\delta_{-\omega\omega^{\prime}})\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta_{\omega^{\prime}\omega}-e^{-\beta E}\delta_{EE^{\prime}}\delta_{-\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}+i\omega^{\prime})}

Taking zero-temperature limit, we obtain

\displaystyle e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega,\omega^{\prime},E=E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E^{\prime}=E_{s}-E_{n})|_{\beta\rightarrow\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg\{-\frac{(\delta_{n,gs}+\delta_{s,gs})}{(E^{\prime}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{E^{\prime}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{E-i\omega}\bigg]-\frac{\delta_{m,gs}-\delta_{s,gs}}{(E-i\omega)(E^{\prime}-E+i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega)}(1-\delta_{E_{m}E_{s}}\delta_{-\omega\omega^{\prime}})\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta_{n,gs}\delta_{\omega^{\prime}\omega}-\delta_{m,gs}\delta_{E_{m}E_{s}}\delta_{-\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E-i\omega^{\prime})(E^{\prime}+i\omega^{\prime})}

and the Fourier transform becomes

\displaystyle-{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg(\frac{1}{-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega)(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(-\Delta_{-1}-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{1}{(-\Delta_{-1}-i\omega)(\Delta_{-2}+i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega)}

(S203)

\displaystyle{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\

Using

S164

\displaystyle I(\omega,-\omega^{\prime},-\omega^{\prime},E=E_{s}-E_{n},E^{\prime}=E_{s}-E_{u},E^{\prime}=E_{s}-E_{u})

(S208)

and the Fourier transform becomes

\displaystyle{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\beta(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega)}\bigg[\frac{1-\delta_{\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}}\delta_{\omega,-\omega^{\prime}}}{(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega)}-\frac{1}{(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}(1+\delta_{\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}}\delta_{\omega,-\omega^{\prime}})\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{{(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]+

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{{(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega)(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega^{\prime})}}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)+1\bigg]

(S215)

Sum
eqs.

S185
,
S191
,
S197
,
S203
,
S209
and
S215
gives the Fourier transform of the four point correlation function

\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2},\tau_{3}}\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{3})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(0)\rangle_{\gamma=0}e^{-i\omega\tau_{3}+i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{2}-i\omega^{\prime}\tau_{1}}=I^{0}_{i\neq j}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})+I_{i\neq j}^{1}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})

(S216)

which has two types of contribution.
The first kind
I^{0}
does not have any
1/\beta
prefactor, which reads:

\displaystyle I^{0}_{i\neq j}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{2i\omega^{\prime}i\omega+(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})-2\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}-(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}

(S217)

Notice that the formula is invariant under
n_{f}\rightarrow N_{f}-n_{f}
together with
\Delta_{+1}\leftrightarrow-\Delta_{-1}
, which is a statement of the particle-hole symmetry in this problem.
The second contribution is proportional to
1/\beta
and is given by

\displaystyle I^{1}_{i\neq j}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}}\frac{2\Delta_{1}-\Delta_{2}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{2}}+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}}\frac{2\Delta_{1}-\Delta_{2}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{4\Delta_{1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{1})^{2}}\bigg[(i\omega)^{2}(i\omega^{\prime})^{2}+((i\omega)^{2}+(i\omega^{\prime})^{2})\Delta_{1}^{2}-3\Delta_{1}^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}

Here, we have used
\Delta_{-1}=\Delta_{+1}=\Delta_{1}
and
\Delta_{-2}=\Delta_{+2}=\Delta_{2}
to simplify the results.
The details of the summation are given from
eq.

S225
to
eq.

S236
.

Using
section

IV.2
, we then find that
F^{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})
is

\displaystyle F^{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})=-I^{0}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})+G_{f,loc}(i\omega)G_{f,loc}(i\omega^{\prime})-I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})+\mathcal{O}(e^{-\beta\frac{U_{1}}{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}-\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}

\displaystyle-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{(2i\omega^{\prime}i\omega+(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})-2\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}-(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{f}^{2}}\bigg(\frac{n_{f}}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{N_{f}-n_{f}}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{n_{f}}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{N_{f}-n_{f}}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)-I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})+\mathcal{O}(e^{-\beta\frac{U_{1}}{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{n_{f}^{2}}{N_{f}^{2}}-\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\bigg[\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})^{2}}{N_{f}^{2}}-\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}

\displaystyle-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{2i\omega^{\prime}i\omega+(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})-2\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}-(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})n_{f}}{N_{f}^{2}}\frac{2i\omega i\omega^{\prime}+(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})-2\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}-I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})+\mathcal{O}(e^{-\beta\frac{U_{1}}{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg[\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{2i\omega^{\prime}i\omega+(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})-2\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}-I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})+\mathcal{O}(e^{-\beta\frac{U_{1}}{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})-(2i\omega^{\prime}i\omega+(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})-2\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1})}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}-I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})+\mathcal{O}(e^{-\beta\frac{U_{1}}{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(1+N_{f}\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}-I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})+\mathcal{O}(e^{-\beta\frac{U_{1}}{2}})

(S219)

By contrast, ghe equation for
F^{ii}
is already correct to order
\mathcal{O}(e^{-\beta\frac{U_{1}}{2}})
and does not contain a
1/\beta
contribution.

In summary, in the zero-temperature limit, we obtain

\displaystyle F^{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})=-I_{i\neq j}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})+G_{f,loc}(i\omega)G_{f,loc}(i\omega^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(1+N_{f}\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}-I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})+\mathcal{O}(e^{-\beta\frac{U_{1}}{2}})

(S220)

IV.2.6
Additional calculational details

In this subsection, we provide the intermediate steps needed for the evaluation of
F^{ij}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})
.

In the calculation of
F^{ii}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})
, we need the special case of
eq.

S164
in which all three energies are equal:

\displaystyle I(\omega_{1},\omega_{2},\omega_{3},E,E,E)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta(E+i\omega_{1})}\bigg\{\frac{1-\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}}{i\omega_{2}-i\omega_{1}}\bigg[\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{E+i\omega_{3}}-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{E+i\omega_{3}-i\omega_{2}+i\omega_{1}}\bigg]-\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{(E+i\omega_{3})^{2}}-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{(E+i\omega_{2})(E+i\omega_{3})}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta_{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}}{(E+i\omega_{1})(E+i\omega_{3})}-\frac{e^{-\beta E}\delta_{\omega_{2}\omega_{3}}}{(E+i\omega_{1})(E+i\omega_{2})}

(S221)

Hence,

\displaystyle I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E,E,E)=-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{(E+i\omega)}+\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg\}+\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}-\frac{e^{-\beta E}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}

\displaystyle I(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},E,E,E)=\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega}\bigg[\frac{1}{E+i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{E+i\omega}\bigg]-\frac{1+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg\}+\frac{\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}-e^{-\beta E}}{(E+i\omega)(E+i\omega^{\prime})}

(S222)

Therefore,

\displaystyle I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E,E,E)-I(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},E,E,E)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{(E+i\omega)}+\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg\}+\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}-\frac{e^{-\beta E}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega}\bigg[\frac{1}{E+i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{E+i\omega}\bigg]-\frac{1+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg\}-\frac{\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}-e^{-\beta E}}{(E+i\omega)(E+i\omega^{\prime})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{(E+i\omega)}+\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg\}+\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}+e^{-\beta E}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}-\frac{e^{-\beta E}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega}\bigg[\frac{1}{E+i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{E+i\omega}\bigg]-\frac{1+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg(\frac{1}{(E+i\omega)}+\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg)-\frac{1+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega}\bigg[\frac{1}{E+i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{E+i\omega}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}+e^{-\beta E}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}-\frac{e^{-\beta E}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg(\frac{1}{(E+i\omega)}-\frac{\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg)+\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega}\bigg[\frac{1}{E+i\omega^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{E+i\omega}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}+e^{-\beta E}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}-\frac{e^{-\beta E}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg(\frac{1}{(E+i\omega)}-\frac{\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg)-\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}-\frac{\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}-\frac{e^{-\beta E}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{\beta(E+i\omega)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{1}{(E+i\omega)}-\frac{1}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})}-\frac{1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1+e^{-\beta E}}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}-\frac{\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}(1+e^{-\beta E})}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}})(1+e^{-\beta E})}{(E+i\omega^{\prime})(E+i\omega)}

(S223)

In the calculation of
F^{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})
, we need to evaluate

\displaystyle{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}^{-1}\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\\
\ket{u}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\\
\ket{m}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(\omega^{\prime},-\omega,-\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{u},E_{m}-E_{u})

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\\
\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}(1-\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega^{\prime},\omega,E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\\
\ket{u}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\\
\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}(1-\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(-\omega^{\prime},-\omega,\omega^{\prime},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{m}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\\
\ket{u}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{s}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n}e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(\omega,-\omega^{\prime},-\omega^{\prime},E_{s}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{u},E_{s}-E_{u})

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n\\
\ket{s}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\\
\ket{u}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}\end{subarray}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}\ket{n}(1-\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\ket{n})e^{-\beta(E_{n}-E_{gs})}I(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime},E_{s}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n},E_{s}-E_{n})\bigg]

(S224)

where the explicit expressions for the six terms are given in
eqs.

S185
,
S191
,
S197
,
S203
,
S209
and
S215
.
In these expressions,
\Delta_{\pm 1}=E(n_{f}\pm 1)-E(n_{f})
denote the energies of charge
\pm 1
excitations and
\Delta_{\pm 2}=E(n_{f}\pm 2)-E(n_{f})
denote the energies of charge
\pm 2
excitations.
In the zero temperature limit at exact integer filling, they satisfy
\Delta_{+1}\approx\Delta_{-1}=U_{1}/2
,
\Delta_{+2}\approx\Delta_{-2}=2U_{1}
.

We now sum these contributions:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}(\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}})+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}})-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})n_{f}}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}-\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega)}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)-1\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{({\Delta_{+1}}-i\omega^{\prime})({\Delta_{-1}}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{{\Delta_{-1}}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{{\Delta_{+1}}-i\omega}\bigg)-\delta_{\omega^{\prime}\omega}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\frac{1}{-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega}-\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)+1\bigg]

(S225)

After further algebra, this becomes

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})n_{f}}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}-\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega)}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)-1\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{({\Delta_{+1}}-i\omega^{\prime})({\Delta_{-1}}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{{\Delta_{-1}}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{{\Delta_{+1}}-i\omega}\bigg)-\delta_{\omega^{\prime}\omega}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg(\frac{1}{-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega}-\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)+1\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})n_{f}}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}-\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})}+\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega)}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)-1\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{({\Delta_{+1}}-i\omega^{\prime})({\Delta_{-1}}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{{\Delta_{-1}}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{{\Delta_{+1}}-i\omega}\bigg)-\delta_{\omega^{\prime}\omega}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(-\Delta_{+1}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}+i\omega^{\prime})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)+1\bigg]

(S226)

After further simplification,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}-\frac{1}{\beta(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{-1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{-1}+i\omega}+\frac{1}{\Delta_{+1}-i\omega^{\prime}}\bigg)-1\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{-1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega{+}\Delta_{-1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)-\delta_{\omega^{\prime}\omega}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)+1\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{2}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)+2\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg\{\frac{-1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega-\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})}-1\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega+\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}+1\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\right)^{2}

(S227)

We simplify this further:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}\bigg)-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{2}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)+2\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega-\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega+\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\frac{1}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\right)^{2}+

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\right)^{2}

(S228)

We now separate the terms proportional to
1/\beta
from the
\beta
-independent terms.
First, the constant terms are

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg[\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\bigg[-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]+

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg[\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}2\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\frac{1}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg[\left(\frac{2}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}-\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}-\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}\right)\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\frac{1}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\bigg]

\displaystyle=
