\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[2i\omega^{\prime}i\omega+(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega)(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})-2\Delta_{+1}\Delta_{-1}-(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}\delta_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bigg]

(S229)

Notice that this expression is invariant under
n_{f}\rightarrow N_{f}-n_{f}
together with
\Delta_{+1}\leftrightarrow-\Delta_{-1}
, which is a statement of the particle-hole symmetry in this problem.

For the terms proportional to
1/\beta
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg[\frac{2}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega-\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega+\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-\Delta_{+1}}\right)^{2}+

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{\beta(\Delta_{-2}+i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})}\left(\frac{1}{i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}}\right)^{2}

(S230)

which are further simplified as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg[\frac{2(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}-\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega-\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega+\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-2\Delta_{+1}}{(\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime})}\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-2})}\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\left[(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)+(n_{f}-1)\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+2\Delta_{-1}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-2}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}\left[(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)+(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-2\Delta_{+1}}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg[\frac{2(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}-\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega-\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega+\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\left[(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)+(n_{f}-1)\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+2\Delta_{-1}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-2}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}\left[(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)+(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-2\Delta_{+1}}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[2(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-(i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega-\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(i\omega^{\prime}-i\omega+\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}

(S231)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\left[(N_{f}-2n_{f}+1)+(n_{f}-1)\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+2\Delta_{-1}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-2}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}\left[(N_{f}-2n_{f}-1)+(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-2\Delta_{+1}}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\bigg(2i\omega i\omega^{\prime}+(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})-2\Delta_{-1}\Delta_{+1}\bigg)\bigg((i\omega)^{2}+(i\omega^{\prime})^{2}+(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})-2\Delta_{-1}\Delta_{+1}\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\}

(S232)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\left[-1+\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+2\Delta_{-1}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-2}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}\left[1+\frac{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-2\Delta_{+1}}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\bigg(2i\omega i\omega^{\prime}+(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})-2\Delta_{-1}\Delta_{+1}\bigg)\bigg((i\omega)^{2}+(i\omega^{\prime})^{2}+(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})-2\Delta_{-1}\Delta_{+1}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}\bigg\}

(S233)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\frac{2\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{-2}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}\frac{\Delta_{+2}-2\Delta_{+1}}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{1}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\bigg[(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})

\displaystyle\times\bigg(2i\omega i\omega^{\prime}+(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})-2\Delta_{-1}\Delta_{+1}\bigg)\bigg((i\omega)^{2}+(i\omega^{\prime})^{2}+(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})-2\Delta_{-1}\Delta_{+1}\bigg)

\displaystyle+(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}-(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}

(S234)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{-1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}\frac{2\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{-2}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}\frac{\Delta_{+2}-2\Delta_{+1}}{\Delta_{+2}-i\omega-i\omega^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[-2(i\omega)^{2}(i\omega^{\prime})^{2}+2(i\omega)(i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{+1}-\Delta_{-1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})-(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})^{2}(\Delta_{+1}^{2}+\Delta_{-1}^{2})

\displaystyle\qquad-(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime})(\Delta_{-1}-\Delta_{+1})(\Delta_{-1}^{2}+\Delta_{+1}^{2})+4(i\omega)(i\omega^{\prime})\Delta_{-1}\Delta_{+1}+2\Delta_{-1}\Delta_{+1}(\Delta_{-1}^{2}+\Delta_{-1}\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{+1}^{2})\bigg]\bigg\}

(S235)

Finally, using
\Delta_{+1}=\Delta_{-1}=\Delta_{1}
and
\Delta_{+2}=\Delta_{-2}=\Delta_{2}
we simplify the above expression to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{1}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}}\frac{2\Delta_{1}-\Delta_{2}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{2}}+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{1}}{(i\omega-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}}\frac{2\Delta_{1}-\Delta_{2}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\frac{4\Delta_{1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{1})^{2}}\bigg[(i\omega)^{2}(i\omega^{\prime})^{2}+((i\omega)^{2}+(i\omega^{\prime})^{2})\Delta_{1}^{2}-3\Delta_{1}^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}

(S236)

IV.3
Self-energy from the hybridization expansion

We are now in a position to evaluate the self-energy within the hybridization expansion.
At order
\gamma^{2}
, the self-energy is related to
F^{ij}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})
through
section

III.1

\displaystyle\Sigma_{f,ij}^{(1)}(i\omega)=\gamma^{2}\delta_{i,j}\sum_{i\omega^{\prime},i^{\prime}}\Delta(i\omega^{\prime})F^{ii^{\prime}}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})\frac{1}{[G_{f,loc}(i\omega)]^{2}}

(S237)

We first perform the sum over
i^{\prime}
.
Using the explicit expressions of
F^{ii}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})
and
F^{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})
obtained in
eqs.

S177
and
S220
,
we find

\displaystyle\sum_{i^{\prime}}F^{ii^{\prime}}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})=

\displaystyle F^{ii}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})+\sum_{i^{\prime}\neq i}F^{ii^{\prime}}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}}\frac{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})}(-1+\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(1+N_{f}\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}})}{{(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{+1})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})}}-(N_{f}-1)I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}}\frac{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}(N_{f}+1)\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}}-(N_{f}-1)I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime}).

(S238)

The local Green’s function
G_{f,loc}(i\omega)
is given by (
eq.

S119
)

\displaystyle G_{f,loc}(i\omega)=\frac{i\omega+\Delta_{-1}-\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})}{(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})}

(S239)

f
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{i^{\prime}}F^{ii^{\prime}}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})\frac{1}{[G_{f,loc}(i\omega)]^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}}\frac{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}(N_{f}+1)\delta_{\omega,\omega^{\prime}}}{\left[i\omega+\Delta_{-1}-\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})\right]^{2}}-\frac{(N_{f}-1)I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}{\left[i\omega+\Delta_{-1}-\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})\right]^{2}}

(S240)

We now define another notation for the
1/\beta
term

\displaystyle L^{1}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime})=\frac{(N_{f}-1)I^{1}_{i\neq j}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime},i\omega^{\prime})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}}{\left[i\omega+\Delta_{-1}-\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})\right]^{2}}

(S241)

where the numerator is

\displaystyle(N_{f}-1)I^{1}_{i\neq j}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}}\frac{(i\omega-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{1})}{(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}}\frac{2\Delta_{1}-\Delta_{2}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}}\frac{(i\omega+\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{1})}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}}\frac{2\Delta_{1}-\Delta_{2}}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}}\frac{4\Delta_{1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}}\bigg[(i\omega)^{2}(i\omega^{\prime})^{2}+((i\omega)^{2}+(i\omega^{\prime})^{2})\Delta_{1}^{2}-3\Delta_{1}^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}

To proceed, we now substitute
\Delta_{2}=4\Delta_{1}

\displaystyle(N_{f}-1)I^{1}_{i\neq j}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{2\Delta_{1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}}\bigg\{\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}}(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{1})^{2}\frac{(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{1})}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+4\Delta_{1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{1})^{2}\frac{(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{1})}{i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-4\Delta_{1}}

\displaystyle+2\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}}\bigg[(i\omega)^{2}(i\omega^{\prime})^{2}+((i\omega)^{2}+(i\omega^{\prime})^{2})\Delta_{1}^{2}-3\Delta_{1}^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}

After further simplification, we obtain

\displaystyle(N_{f}-1)I^{1}_{i\neq j}(\omega,\omega^{\prime},\omega^{\prime})(i\omega+\Delta_{-1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N_{f}}\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{2\Delta_{1}}{(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+4\Delta_{1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-4\Delta_{1})}

\displaystyle\times\bigg\{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)(i\omega^{\prime}-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega-\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega+2\Delta_{1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-4\Delta_{1})

\displaystyle\qquad+(N_{f}-n_{f})(N_{f}-n_{f}-1)(i\omega^{\prime}+\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega+\Delta_{1})^{2}(i\omega^{\prime}+i\omega-2\Delta_{1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+4\Delta_{1})

\displaystyle\qquad+2n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})\bigg[(i\omega)^{2}(i\omega^{\prime})^{2}+((i\omega)^{2}+(i\omega^{\prime})^{2})\Delta_{1}^{2}-3\Delta_{1}^{4}\bigg](i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}-4\Delta_{1})(i\omega+i\omega^{\prime}+4\Delta_{1})\bigg\}

(S244)

Using the results above, we obtain the self-energy to order
\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle\Sigma_{f,ij}^{(1)}(i\omega)=\gamma^{2}\delta_{i,j}\Delta(i\omega){\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}}\frac{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{\left[i\omega+\Delta_{-1}-\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})\right]^{2}}-\gamma^{2}\delta_{i,j}\sum_{i\omega^{\prime}}\Delta(i\omega^{\prime})L^{1}(i\omega,i\omega^{\prime})

(S245)

Notice that the local self-energy (
eq.

S120
) is

\displaystyle\Sigma_{f,loc}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})U_{1}^{2}}{N_{f}^{2}}\bigg[\frac{1}{i\omega-\frac{n_{f}\Delta_{+1}-(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}}{N_{f}}}\bigg]

(S246)

Thus, if we ignore the
1/\beta
contribution to the self-energy, the total self-energy up to the order of
\gamma^{2}
behaves as

\displaystyle\Sigma_{f,ij}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})\approx

\displaystyle\delta_{i,j}\Sigma_{f,loc}(i\omega)+\Sigma_{f,ij}^{(1)}(i\omega)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i,j}\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})n_{f}U_{1}^{2}}{N_{f}^{2}}\frac{1}{i\omega-\frac{n_{f}\Delta_{+1}-(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}}{N_{f}}}\bigg[1+{\frac{\gamma^{2}N_{f}}{U_{1}^{2}}}\Delta(i\omega)\frac{{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}}+{\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}{N_{f}}}}{i\omega-\frac{n_{f}\Delta_{+1}-(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}}{N_{f}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i,j}\Sigma_{f,loc}(i\omega)\bigg[1+{\frac{\gamma^{2}N_{f}}{U_{1}^{2}}}\Delta(i\omega)\frac{{(\Delta_{-1}+\Delta_{+1})^{2}}+{\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}{N_{f}}}}{i\omega-\frac{n_{f}\Delta_{+1}-(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}}{N_{f}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\delta_{i,j}\Sigma_{f,loc}(i\omega)\bigg[1+{\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{U_{1}^{2}}}\Delta(i\omega)\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}{i\omega-\frac{n_{f}\Delta_{+1}-(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}}{N_{f}}}\bigg]}

(S247)

IV.4
Hybridization function

The self-energy derived in the previous subsection depends explicitly on the hybridization function
\Delta(i\omega)
. In this subsection, we derive an analytic expression for
\Delta(i\omega)
.

In
appendix

III
,
H^{(c,\eta)}
and
H^{(cf,\eta)}
are defined as

\displaystyle H^{(c,\eta)}({\mathbf{k}})=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{0}(\epsilon_{c,1}-\mu)&v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}+ik_{y}\sigma_{z})\\
v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}-ik_{y}\sigma_{z})&\sigma_{0}(\epsilon_{c,2}-\mu)+M\sigma_{x}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle H^{(cf,\eta)}({\mathbf{k}})=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\gamma\sigma_{0}+v_{\star}^{\prime}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{x}+k_{y}\sigma_{y})\\
0_{2\times 2}\end{pmatrix}

(S248)

where (
eq.

S49
)

\displaystyle\epsilon_{c,1}=

\displaystyle\frac{V(0)}{\Omega_{0}}\nu_{c}+W_{1}\nu_{f}

(S249)

\displaystyle\epsilon_{c,2}=

\displaystyle\frac{V(0)}{\Omega_{0}}\nu_{c}+W_{3}\nu_{f}-\frac{J}{8}\nu_{f}

(S250)

Based on the analytic expression of the hybridization function
eq.

S53
, we analyze the analytic behaviors of the self-energy.
To obtain a closed analytic form, we make the following approximations:
1) We set
W_{1}=W_{3}
and ignore the HF contribution of
H_{J}
to the
c
electrons, which yields
\epsilon_{c,1}=\epsilon_{c,2}
(Note that the HF contribution of
H_{J}
to the
f
electrons is still kept).
2) We focus on the integer fillings
\nu=0,\pm 1,\pm 2
where, in the zero-hybridization limit, we have
\nu_{f}=n_{f}-N_{f}/2=\nu
and
\nu_{c}=0
.
This will fix the chemical potential to
\mu=\epsilon_{c,1}=\epsilon_{c,2}
.
3) We will take the ”chiral limit U(
4
) symmetry ” of the THF model with
v_{\star}^{\prime}=0
(note, however, that the parameters used in the final comparison are chosen
away
from the chiral limit, to match the BM model
174
).

Under the approximations, the mean-field Hamiltonian for
c
electrons reduces to

\displaystyle H_{c}=\sum_{{\mathbf{k}},\eta s}\begin{bmatrix}c_{{\mathbf{k}},1\eta s}^{\dagger}&c_{{\mathbf{k}},2\eta s}^{\dagger}&c_{{\mathbf{k}},3\eta s}^{\dagger}&c_{{\mathbf{k}},4\eta s}^{\dagger}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}&&v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})\\
&&&v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})\\
v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})&&&M\\
&v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})&M\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}c_{{\mathbf{k}},1\eta s}\\
c_{{\mathbf{k}},2\eta s}\\
c_{{\mathbf{k}},3\eta s}\\
c_{{\mathbf{k}},4\eta s}\end{bmatrix}

(S251)

The corresponding eigenvalues and eigenvectors are

\displaystyle E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1,2,3,4}=\frac{-M-\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2},\frac{M-\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2},\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2},\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}

\displaystyle U^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},an=1}=A_{1,{\mathbf{k}}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})&\frac{M-\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})&-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}&|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{a}

\displaystyle U^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},an=2}=A_{2,{\mathbf{k}}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{-M-\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})&\frac{-M-\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})&|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}&|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{a}

\displaystyle U^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},an=3}=A_{2,{\mathbf{k}}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{-M-\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})&\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})&-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}&|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{a}

\displaystyle U^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},an=4}=A_{1,{\mathbf{k}}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})&\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})&|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}&|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{a}

(S252)

where the normalization factors are

\displaystyle A_{1,{\mathbf{k}}}=\frac{1}{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}-M\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}}

\displaystyle A_{2,{\mathbf{k}}}=\frac{1}{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}}

(S253)

To emphasize the dependence on the parameter
M
, we denote the corresponding hybridization function by
\Delta_{M}(i\omega)
.
Using
eqs.

S53
and
S97
m], the hybridization function reads

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\Delta_{M}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\sum_{n,a_{1}a_{2}}[H^{(fc,\eta)}({\mathbf{k}})]_{\alpha a_{1}}U^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},a_{1}n}\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}U^{\eta,c,*}_{{\mathbf{k}},a_{2}n}[H^{(fc,\eta)}({\mathbf{k}})]^{*}_{\alpha a_{2}}\bigg|_{\alpha=1\;\mathrm{or}\;2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\sum_{n}\frac{|U^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},\alpha n}|^{2}}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}\bigg|_{\alpha=1\;\mathrm{or}\;2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\bigg[|A_{1,{\mathbf{k}}}|^{2}\bigg(\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}\bigg)^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+|A_{2,{\mathbf{k}}}|^{2}\bigg(\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}\bigg)^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\frac{(\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}-M)^{2}}{\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}(\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}-M)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\frac{(\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}+M)^{2}}{\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}(\sqrt{4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}}+M)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\bigg(1+\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}}\bigg)\bigg]

(S254)

where the restriction
|_{\alpha=1\;\mathrm{or}\;2}
indicates that only the
\alpha=1,2
components couple directly to the
f
orbitals.
Note that the
c
-electron spectrum is particle-hole symmetric at integer fillings of the entire system in the absence of
fc
hybridization
\gamma
, i.e.
E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}=-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}
and
E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}=-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}
. At these fillings, the
c
electron is half filled, and the heavy
f
electrons control the charge number.
Using
\mathrm{Im}[\frac{1}{\omega-i0^{+}-E}]=\pi\delta(\omega-E)
, we introduce the following spectral density associated with the hybridization function

\displaystyle\rho_{c}^{hyb}(\epsilon)\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi}\mathrm{Im}\bigg[\Delta_{M}(i\omega\rightarrow\epsilon-i0^{+})\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\Omega_{M}}\int_{|{\mathbf{k}}|<\Lambda_{c}}\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)[\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1})+\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4})]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\bigg(1+\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)[\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2})+\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3})]\bigg]d^{2}k

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\pi\gamma^{2}}{\Omega_{M}}\int_{0}^{\Lambda_{c}}\frac{1}{4}\bigg[\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}k|^{2}}}\bigg)\delta(|\epsilon|-\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2})

\displaystyle+\bigg(1+\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}k|^{2}}}\bigg)\delta(|\epsilon|-\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2})\bigg]kdk

(S255)

To evaluate the first delta-function contribution, we introduce the variable
u=\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}\in[M,\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}]
, then
\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}=2u-M
,
4|v_{\star}|^{2}k^{2}=(2u-M)^{2}-M^{2}
and
8|v_{\star}|^{2}kdk=4(2u-M)du
.
There the integral is evaluated as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\Lambda_{c}}\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}k|^{2}}}\bigg)\delta(|\epsilon|-\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2})kdk

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{M}^{\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}}(1-\frac{M}{2u-M})\delta(|\epsilon|-u)\frac{(2u-M)du}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|v_{\star}|^{2}}\int_{M}^{\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}}\delta(|\epsilon|-u)(u-M)du

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|v_{\star}|^{2}}(|\epsilon|-M)\theta(|\epsilon|-M)\theta(\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}-|\epsilon|)

(S256)

Similarly, for the term involving
\delta(|\epsilon|-\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2})
, one simply replaces
M
by
-M
in
eq.

S256
.
Substituting
eq.

S256
and the
M\to-M
counterpart into
section

IV.4
, we obtain

\displaystyle\rho_{c}^{hyb}(\epsilon)=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\bigg[(|\epsilon|-M)\theta(|\epsilon|-M)\theta(\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}-|\epsilon|)+(|\epsilon|+M)\theta(\frac{\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}-M}{2}-|\epsilon|)\bigg]

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\bigg[(|\epsilon|-M)\theta(|\epsilon|-M)+(|\epsilon|+M)\bigg]\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\bigg[(|\epsilon|-M)(1-\theta(M-|\epsilon|))+(|\epsilon|+M)\bigg]\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}|\epsilon|\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)+(M-|\epsilon|)\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\theta(M-|\epsilon|)

(S257)

In the last step, we have used the fact that
|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|\gg M
.
Thus,
\theta(\frac{\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}\pm M}{2}-|\epsilon|)\approx\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)
and
\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)\theta(M-|\epsilon|)=\theta(M-|\epsilon|)
.

The hybridization function can then be reconstructed from its spectral density through (note that
\mathrm{Im}[\frac{1}{\omega-i0^{+}-E}]=\pi\delta(\omega-E)
)

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\Delta_{M}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle\int_{\epsilon}\rho^{hyb}_{c}(\epsilon)\frac{d\epsilon}{i\omega-\epsilon}

(S258)

using
eq.

S257
, we obtain

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\Delta_{M}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle\int\bigg[\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}|\epsilon|\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)+(M-|\epsilon|)\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\theta(M-|\epsilon|)\bigg]\frac{d\epsilon}{i\omega-\epsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\int_{-|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|}^{|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|}\frac{|\epsilon|d\epsilon}{i\omega-\epsilon}+\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\int_{-M}^{M}\frac{(M-|\epsilon|)d\epsilon}{i\omega-\epsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\int_{0}^{|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|}d\epsilon\bigg(\frac{\epsilon}{i\omega-\epsilon}+\frac{\epsilon}{i\omega+\epsilon}\bigg)+\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\int_{0}^{M}d\epsilon\bigg(\frac{M-\epsilon}{i\omega-\epsilon}+\frac{M-\epsilon}{i\omega+\epsilon}\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}[-i\omega\log(\epsilon-i\omega)-i\omega\log(\epsilon+i\omega)]\bigg|_{\epsilon=0}^{|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|}

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}[(i\omega-M)\log(\epsilon-i\omega)+(i\omega+M)\log(\epsilon+i\omega)]\bigg|_{\epsilon=0}^{M}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\omega\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}M\log\bigg(
\frac{i\omega+M }{i\omega-M}\bigg)}+{i\omega\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{M^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)}

(S259)

For later convenience, we define

\displaystyle f_{M}(i\omega)=\log\bigg( \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2+\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg)-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\frac{M}{2i\omega}\log\bigg( \frac{i\omega+M}{i\omega-M}
\bigg)}}{-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\frac{M^2+\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg)}

(S260)

such that

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\Delta_{M}(i\omega)=-i\omega\kappa\pi f_{M}(i\omega)

(S261)

where we introduced the dimensionless parameter

\kappa=\frac{\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}

(S262)

to measure the hybridization strength.

After analytic continuation
i\omega\to\omega+i0^{+}
, the real-frequency expression reads

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\Delta_{M}(\omega+i0^{+})=-\omega\kappa\pi f_{M}(\omega+i0^{+})

(S263)

with

\displaystyle f_{M}(i\omega\to\omega+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle\log[\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}-(\omega+i0^{+})^{2}}{-(\omega+i0^{+})^{2}}\bigg]-\frac{M}{2(\omega+i0^{+})}\log[\frac{(\omega+i0^{+})+M}{(\omega+i0^{+})-M}\bigg]-\frac{1}{2}\log[\frac{M^{2}-(\omega+i0^{+})^{2}}{-(\omega+i0^{+})^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log[\frac{\omega^{2}-|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}}{\omega^{2}}+i\mathrm{sgn}(\omega)0^{+}\bigg]-\frac{M}{2\omega}\log\bigg(\frac{\omega+M}{\omega-M}-i0^+\bigg)-\frac{1}{2}\log[\frac{\omega^{2}-M^{2}}{\omega^{2}}+i\mathrm{sgn}(\omega)0^{+}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{M}{2\omega}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{\omega+M}{\omega-M}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2-\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)

\displaystyle+i\pi\mathrm{sgn}(\omega)\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\omega|)+i\frac{\pi}{2}\frac{M}{\omega}\theta(M-|\omega|)-i\frac{\pi}{2}\mathrm{sgn}(\omega)\theta(M-|\omega|)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{M}{2\omega}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{\omega+M}{\omega-M}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2-\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)

\displaystyle+i\pi\mathrm{sgn}(\omega)\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\omega|)+i\frac{\pi}{2}\frac{M-|\omega|}{\omega}\theta(M-|\omega|)

(S264)

To evaluate the logarithms unambiguously, we choose the branch cut of
\log(z)
to be
z\in(-\infty,0]
.
Accordingly, for
x\in\mathbb{R}
, we have
\mathrm{Im}[\log(x\pm i0^+)]=\pm\pi\theta(-x)
.

In the chiral-flat limit
M=0
, the hybridization function reduces to

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\Delta(i\omega)=\gamma^{2}\Delta_{M}(i\omega)\bigg|_{M=0}=-i\omega\pi\kappa f(i\omega)

(S265)

with

\displaystyle f(i\omega)=f_{M}(i\omega)\bigg|_{M=0}=\log\bigg(\frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2 } \bigg)

(S266)

The corresponding real-frequency expression is

\displaystyle f(\omega+i0^{+})=\log\bigg( \bigg|
\frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 -\omega^2}{\omega^2 }\bigg|
\bigg)+i\pi\text{sgn}(\omega)\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\omega|)

(S267)

IV.4.1
Symmetries of the hybridization function

As shown in
eq.

S55
to
eq.

S58
, the flavor U(8)-symmetric local hybridization function
\Delta(i\omega)
keeps the spin SU(2), valley U
{}_{V}(1)
,
C_{3z}
,
C_{2x}
and
C_{2z}
symmetries.
Here we show that the hybridization function
\Delta_{M}(i\omega)
derived in
section

IV.4
also preserves the particle-hole symmetry and time-reversal symmetry.

The local self-energy
eq.

S120
respects the particle-hole symmetry
P:\Delta_{+1}\leftrightarrow\Delta_{-1},n_{f}\to(N_{f}-n_{f})
as

\displaystyle P\Sigma_{f,loc}(i\omega)P^{-1}=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})U_{1}^{2}}{N_{f}^{2}}\frac{1}{i\omega-\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}-n_{f}\Delta_{+1}}{N_{f}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})U_{1}^{2}}{N_{f}^{2}}\frac{1}{-i\omega-\frac{n_{f}\Delta_{+1}-(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}}{N_{f}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Sigma_{f,loc}(-i\omega)

(S268)

In the flavor space of
f
orbitals, the representation of particle-hole symmetry is
D^{f}(P)=i\sigma_{z}\tau_{z}
(
\sigma
for orbital and
\tau
for valley).
This representation is diagonal in flavor space.
Therefore, the self-energy to order
\gamma^{2}

section

IV.3
will transform under particle-hole symmetry as

\displaystyle P\Sigma_{f,ij}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})P^{-1}=

\displaystyle P\delta_{i,j}\Sigma_{f,loc}(i\omega)\bigg[1+{\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{U_{1}^{2}}}\Delta_{M}(i\omega)\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}{i\omega-\frac{n_{f}\Delta_{+1}-(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}}{N_{f}}}\bigg]P^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i,j}[-\Sigma_{f,loc}(-i\omega)]\bigg[1+{\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{U_{1}^{2}}}\Delta_{M}(i\omega)\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}{i\omega-\frac{(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}-n_{f}\Delta_{+1}}{N_{f}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i,j}[-\Sigma_{f,loc}(-i\omega)]\bigg[1-{\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{U_{1}^{2}}}\Delta_{M}(i\omega)\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}{-i\omega-\frac{n_{f}\Delta_{+1}-(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}}{N_{f}}}\bigg]

(S269)

Since
\Sigma_{f,ij}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})
must also satisfy the particle-hole symmetry
P\Sigma_{f,ij}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})P^{-1}=-\Sigma_{f,ij}(-i\omega,-{\mathbf{k}})
, this puts a constraint on the hybridization function:

\Delta_{M}(-i\omega)=-\Delta_{M}(i\omega)

(S270)

This constraint is indeed satisfied by the explicit expression in
section

IV.4
:

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\Delta_{M}(-i\omega)=

\displaystyle i\omega\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)+\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}M\log\bigg(
\frac{-i\omega+M }{-i\omega-M}\bigg)-{i\omega\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{M^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\omega\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)-\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}M\log\bigg(
\frac{i\omega+M }{i\omega-M}\bigg)-{i\omega\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{M^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\gamma^{2}\Delta_{M}(i\omega)

(S271)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}[\Delta_{M}(i\omega)]^{*}=

\displaystyle i\omega\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)+\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}M\log\bigg(
\frac{-i\omega+M }{-i\omega-M}\bigg)-{i\omega\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{2|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{M^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\Delta_{M}(-i\omega)

(S272)

Therefore, the self-energy also respects time-reversal symmetry:

\displaystyle T\Sigma_{f,ij}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})T^{-1}=

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{f,ij}(i\omega,-{\mathbf{k}})]^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i,j}[\Sigma_{f,loc}(i\omega)]^{*}\bigg[1+{\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{U_{1}^{2}}}(\Delta_{M}(i\omega))^{*}\frac{(\Delta_{+1}+\Delta_{-1})^{2}}{-i\omega-\frac{n_{f}\Delta_{+1}-(N_{f}-n_{f})\Delta_{-1}}{N_{f}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Sigma_{f,ij}(-i\omega,-{\mathbf{k}})

(S273)

Appendix V
Numerical results

using the self-energy derived in
section

IV.3
, we can also obtain the Green’s function and the corresponding spectral functions numerically.
In
fig.

S4
, we illustrate the spectral function with the Hubbard-I approximation (
eq.

S246
) and the result from the hybridization expansion (
section

IV.3
) at integer filling
\nu=0,-1,-2
.
In
fig.

S4
, we have used the full model with finite
M
and
v_{\star}^{\prime}
.

Several general remarks are in order. First, the self-energy at order
\gamma^{2}

renormalizes the Hubbard-band energies, shifting them toward the Fermi level.
Second, the self-energy introduces finite damping of the Hubbard bands.
These trends are qualitatively consistent with the DMFT results reported in
Ref.
154
.
In addition, both Hubbard bands at
\nu=0
, the lower Hubbard bands at
\nu=-1
and
\nu=-2
are strongly damped
and are barely seen in the figure, again in qualitative agreement with the DMFT results
of Ref.
154
.

Figure S4:
Spectral functions at filling
\nu=0,-1,-2
obtained from Hubbard-I approximation
eq.

S246
and hybridization expansions
section

IV.3
. We take the same parameters as in Ref.
154
. In generating the Hubbard-I spectral functions, we introduce an artificial damping
\delta=0.1\,\mathrm{meV}
to both
f
and
c
electrons, which amounts to replacing each Dirac delta function,
\delta(x)
, in the spectral function with a Lorentzian broadening
\frac{\delta/\pi}{x^{2}+\delta^{2}}
. Note that in the hybridization expansion (or the infinite
s\equiv\kappa
-sum series methods
110
;
193
;
202
, if the original
\kappa\equiv s
parameter is kept, the Hubbard bands are broadened too much. A reduction of
\kappa\equiv s
by a factor of 4 is necessary to match DMFT, which in its turn matches QTM. This might be due to the fact that
(N_{f}+1)\pi\kappa^{2}\sim 1
and hence higher orders are needed for these expansions to converge.

Appendix VI
Self-energy and spectral function at charge neutrality in the chiral limit (
v_{\star}^{\prime}=0
)

In this section, we analytically investigate the properties of the spectral
function and the self-energy at charge neutrality (
\nu=0
) in the chiral limit
with
v_{\star}^{\prime}=0
. We consider two cases separately, corresponding to
M=0

and
M\neq 0
. We first focus on the case
M=0
.

At charge neutrality, we have
n_{f}=N_{f}/2
with
N_{f}=8
. The charge
\pm 1
excitation energy reads (
eq.

S118
)

\displaystyle\Delta_{\pm 1}=\frac{U_{1}}{2}

(S274)

The self-energy now behaves as (from
section

IV.3
)

\displaystyle\Sigma_{f,ij}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})\approx\delta_{i,j}\Sigma^{CNP}(i\omega)

(S275)

where

\displaystyle\Sigma_{f}^{CNP}(i\omega)=\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4i\omega}\bigg[1+{\frac{\gamma^{2}}{U_{1}^{2}}\Delta(i\omega)\frac{U_{1}^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{i\omega}}\bigg]

(S276)

We first consider the chiral-flat limit with
v_{\star}^{\prime}=0,M=0
. The hybridization function is given in
eq.

S265
. Then

\displaystyle\Sigma^{CNP}_{f}(i\omega)={\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4i\omega}\bigg[1-(N_{f}+1)\pi\kappa f(i\omega)\bigg]}

(S277)

We rewrite
\Sigma_{f}^{CNP}(i\omega)
by introducing
Z_{a}(i\omega)

\displaystyle\Sigma_{f}^{CNP}(i\omega)=\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)}{4i\omega}

\displaystyle Z_{a}(i\omega)=1-(N_{f}+1)\pi\kappa f(i\omega)=1-(N_{f}+1)\frac{\pi\gamma^{2}}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg(\frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2 }\bigg)

(S278)

The Hubbard-I self-energy is recovered in the limit of
\gamma\to 0
and
Z_{a}(i\omega)=1
.
While in the following, we will keep the entire
Z_{a}(i\omega)
including the contribution to the order of
\gamma^{2}
.
This term will have imaginary part in the real frequency
i\omega\to\omega+i0^{+}
, which will introduce a finite lifetime to the
c
-electron.

We note that, with only the Hubbard-I self-energy, one can introduce an auxiliary fermion to rewrite the Hubbard-I Green’s function, as shown in
81
;
13
.
To get an analytic expression of the poles of the Green’s function, we now take a similar approach.
This approach is equivalent to solve poles of Green’s function directly.
However, by using the auxiliary fermion approach, it is more easy to compare with Hubbard I results.
Here, we introduce an auxiliary fermion
a_{{\mathbf{k}},\alpha\eta s}
for each flavor of the
f
electron at each site. We define the Green’s function as

\displaystyle[G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(\tau,{\mathbf{k}})]_{ij}=-\langle T_{\tau}[\psi_{{\mathbf{k}},\alpha\eta s}]_{i}(\tau)[\psi_{{\mathbf{k}},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}]_{j}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle\psi_{{\mathbf{k}},\alpha\eta s}=\begin{bmatrix}f_{{\mathbf{k}},\alpha\eta s}&c_{{\mathbf{k}},\alpha\eta s}&c_{{\mathbf{k}},(\alpha+2)\eta s}&a_{{\mathbf{k}},\alpha\eta s}\end{bmatrix}

(S279)

which takes the form of

\displaystyle[G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}i\omega&-\gamma&0&-\frac{U_{1}}{2}\\
-\gamma&i\omega&-v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}+i(-1)^{\alpha+1}k_{y})&0\\
0&-v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}-i(-1)^{\alpha+1}k_{y})&i\omega&0\\
-\frac{U_{1}}{2}&0&0&Z^{-1}_{a}(i\omega)i\omega\end{bmatrix}

(S280)

Note that the above equation is the definition of the Green’s function of the system with the auxiliary degrees of freedom.
We can observe that, by letting
Z_{a}(i\omega)=1
, we recover the effective non-interacting Green’s function of the auxiliary system under Hubbard-I approximation.

By taking the inverse of the matrix in
appendix

VI
, we obtain the following Green’s function for the
f
electron.

\displaystyle[G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{11}=\frac{1}{i\omega-\gamma^{2}\Delta_{{\mathbf{k}}}^{\alpha\eta s,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega)-\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)}{4i\omega}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{i\omega-\gamma^{2}\Delta_{{\mathbf{k}}}^{\alpha\eta s,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega)-\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)}{4i\omega}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{i\omega-\gamma^{2}\Delta_{{\mathbf{k}}}^{\alpha\eta s,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega)-\Sigma^{CNP}(i\omega)}

(S281)

which is exactly the same interacting Green’s function of
f
electron that we have derived (see
eqs.

S63
,
S64
and
VI
.
We emphasize that the introduction of
G^{{aux},\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})
(
appendix

VI
) is merely another way to present the interacting Green’s function of the system.
From
appendix

VI
, we also observe that, at order
\gamma^{2}
, the self-energy generates an effective quasiparticle weight renormalization
Z_{a}(i\omega)
for both the auxiliary fermion.

We now consider the trace of Green’s function for the physical degrees of freedom

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=\text{Tr}[G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})P_{phys}]

(S282)

where the projection matrix only keeps the physical degrees of freedom
P_{phys}=\text{diag}\{1,1,1,0\}
.
The spectral function of the system is then defined as

\displaystyle\rho^{\alpha\eta s}(\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=-\frac{1}{\pi}\mathrm{Im}\bigg[\mathcal{G}^{\alpha\eta s}(i\omega\rightarrow\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}})\bigg]

(S283)

We now investigate the Green’s function. The Green’s function takes the form of

\displaystyle G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=\text{adj}([G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1})/\text{det}([G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1})

(S284)

where
adj
denotes the adjugate of the matrix.
The determinant of the inverse of Green’s function matrix (
appendix

VI
) reads

\displaystyle\text{det}([G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}))]^{-1}]=\frac{1}{Z_{a}(i\omega)}\bigg[(i\omega)^{4}-(i\omega)^{2}\bigg(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+(\gamma^{2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)}{4})\bigg)+\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)}{4}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\bigg]

(S285)

Again, in the
Z_{a}(i\omega)=1
limit, we recover the Hubbard-I limit, and the determinant simply reads

\displaystyle\text{det}([G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}))]^{-1}]=

\displaystyle\bigg[i\omega-\sqrt{\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\gamma^{2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4}-\sqrt{\bigg(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\gamma^{2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4}\bigg)^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\bigg[i\omega+\sqrt{\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\gamma^{2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4}-\sqrt{\bigg(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\gamma^{2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4}\bigg)^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\bigg[i\omega-\sqrt{\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\gamma^{2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4}+\sqrt{\bigg(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\gamma^{2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4}\bigg)^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\bigg[i\omega+\sqrt{\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\gamma^{2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4}+\sqrt{\bigg(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\gamma^{2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{4}\bigg)^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}{2}}\bigg]

(S286)

which can be understood as
\prod_{n}(i\omega-E_{{\mathbf{k}},n})
where
E_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
describes the dispersion of the Hubbard-I bands.
After introducing
Z_{a}(i\omega)
,
U_{1}^{2}
is effectively renormalized to
U_{1}^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)
(from
eq.

S285
). We therefore replace
U_{1}^{2}
by
U_{1}^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)
in
appendix

VI
, while also introducing an additional prefactor
1/Z_{a}(i\omega)
, as shown in
eq.

S285
. This leads to

\displaystyle\text{det}([G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}))]^{-1}]=\frac{1}{Z_{a}(i\omega)}\prod_{n=1}^{4}\bigg[i\omega-\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg]

(S287)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=1}=\sqrt{\frac{\gamma^{2}+Z_{a}(i\omega)U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}-\sqrt{[\gamma^{2}+Z_{a}(i\omega)U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}]^{2}-Z_{a}(i\omega)U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}{2}}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=2}=\sqrt{\frac{\gamma^{2}+Z_{a}(i\omega)U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\sqrt{[\gamma^{2}+Z_{a}(i\omega)U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}]^{2}-Z_{a}(i\omega)U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}{2}}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=3}=-\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=1}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=4}=-\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=2}

(S288)

Then the decomposition of the determinant (
eq.

S287
) allows us to rewrite the trace of Green’s function as (also using
eq.

S284
)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})

\displaystyle=\text{Tr}[G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})P_{phys}]=\frac{1}{\text{det}([G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1})}\text{Tr}\bigg[\text{adj}([G^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1})P_{phys}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1,...,4}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}}(-1)^{n+1}}{i\omega-\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}}

(S289)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}}=\frac{2\bigg(2[\gamma^{2}+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}-3(i\omega)^{2}]+U_{1}^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)\bigg)}{Z_{a}(i\omega)\sqrt{[4\gamma^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)]^{2}-16U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}Z_{a}(i\omega)}}

(S290)

Such a decomposition characterizes the dispersion/spectral functions of the interacting system, as can be seen by revisiting the Hubbard–I approximation. Within the Hubbard-I approximation, we have
Z_{a}(i\omega)=1
and we recover the Hubbard-I green’s function discussed in Ref.
81
taking the form of

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})\overset{\mathrm{Hubbard\,I}}{=}\sum_{n=1,...,4}\frac{A_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}}(-1)^{n+1}}{i\omega-E_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}

\displaystyle E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}=\sqrt{\frac{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}-\sqrt{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}{2}}

\displaystyle E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}=\sqrt{\frac{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\sqrt{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}{2}}

\displaystyle E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}=-E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}

\displaystyle E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}=-E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}

\displaystyle A_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}}=\frac{2\bigg(2[\gamma^{2}+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}-3(i\omega)^{2}]+U_{1}^{2}\bigg)}{\sqrt{[4\gamma^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+U_{1}^{2}]^{2}-16U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}

(S291)

The Green’s function within the Hubbard-I approximation has four poles (in the real frequency) located at
E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1/2/3/4}
describing the corresponding excitation for each
\alpha\eta s
sector as discussed in Ref.
81
.

We next consider the correction introduced by
Z_{a}(i\omega)
. By treating
\kappa
as a small parameter, we find

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=1}\approx E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}\bigg[1-(N_{f}+1)\pi\kappa f(i\omega)\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{16E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}^{2}}\bigg(1-\frac{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})}{\sqrt{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=2}\approx E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}\bigg[1-(N_{f}+1)\pi\kappa f(i\omega)\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{16E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}^{2}}\bigg(1+\frac{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})}{\sqrt{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg]

(S292)

We now work in the real-frequency to obtain the spectral function. In the real frequency, we notice that
f(\omega+i0^{+})
(see
eq.

S267
)

\displaystyle f(\omega+i0^{+})=\log\bigg( \bigg|
\frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 -\omega^2}{\omega^2 }\bigg|
\bigg)+i\pi\text{sgn}(\omega)\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\omega|)

(S293)

is a complex number for given
\omega
. Thus
\mathcal{E}_{\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}},n}
is also complex. We separate the
\mathcal{E}_{\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}},n}
into real and imaginary part such that

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}},n}=\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}-i{\Gamma}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}

(S294)

The Green’s function can then be expressed as

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{\alpha\eta s}(\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}})=\sum_{n=1,...,4}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}}}(-1)^{n+1}}{\omega-\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}+i{\Gamma}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}}

(S295)

The spectral functions are

\displaystyle\rho^{\alpha\eta s}(\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=-\frac{1}{\pi}\mathrm{Im}[\mathcal{G}^{\alpha\eta s}(\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}})]=-\sum_{n=1,...,4}\frac{(-1)^{n+1}\mathrm{Im}[\mathcal{A}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}}}(\omega-\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}+i{\Gamma}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})]}{(\omega-\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})^{2}+({\Gamma}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})^{2}}

(S296)

We can then define the renormalized dispersion and the damping rate from the denominator
(\omega-\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})^{2}+(\Gamma_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})^{2}
.
The renormalized dispersion
\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
is defined as the solution of

\displaystyle\bigg[\omega-\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg]\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}=0

(S297)

The corresponding damping rate can be defined as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}={\Gamma}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}

(S298)

We can write the renormalized dispersion as a series of
\kappa

\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}=\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}^{(0)}+\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}^{(1)}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S299)

where the superscript
(i)
means the
i
-th order of
\kappa
.
Then up to first order of
\kappa
,
eq.

S297
becomes

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}+\tilde{E}^{(1)}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}+(\partial_{\omega}\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}\times\tilde{E}^{(1)}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S300)

Comparing the zeroth order term we find that

\tilde{E}^{(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}=E_{{\mathbf{k}},n}

(S301)

which is nothing but the Hubbard-I dispersion.
On the other hand, we have

\partial_{\omega}\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\propto\kappa\partial_{\omega}\mathrm{Re}f(\omega+i0^{+})=\kappa\frac{2|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}}{\omega(\omega^{2}-|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2})}

(S302)

is already first order of
\kappa
, thus up to first order of
\kappa
we have

\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}=\tilde{E}^{(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}+\tilde{E}^{(1)}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})=\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})=\mathcal{R}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega={E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S303)

To be specific,

\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}\approx

\displaystyle E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}\bigg[1-(N_{f}+1)\pi\kappa[\mathrm{Re}f(E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}+i0^{+})]\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{16E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}^{2}}\bigg(1-\frac{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})}{\sqrt{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}\approx

\displaystyle E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}\bigg[1-(N_{f}+1)\pi\kappa[\mathrm{Re}f(E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}+i0^{+})]\frac{U_{1}^{2}}{16E_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}^{2}}\bigg(1+\frac{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})}{\sqrt{(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})^{2}-U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}=

\displaystyle-\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}

\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}=

\displaystyle-\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}

(S304)

We are particularly interested in two types of features. First, we focus on the Hubbard band located near the Brillouin-zone boundary, described by
\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}
. Second, we examine the gapless excitations in the vicinity of the
\Gamma
point, described by
\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}
.

Near the Brillouin zone edge, we can take
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|=|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|
, where
\Lambda_{c}=\sqrt{\Omega_{M}/\pi}
. We observe that

\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}\bigg|_{|{\mathbf{k}}|=\Lambda_{c}}\approx\frac{U_{1}}{2}\bigg[1-\frac{1}{2}\kappa\pi(N_{f}+1)\log( \frac{4 \gamma^2}{\pi\kappa U_1^2} )\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S305)

which describes the energy renormalization of the Hubbard bands.
The corresponding scattering rate reads

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}\bigg|_{|{\mathbf{k}}|=\Lambda_{c}}\approx\frac{U_{1}\pi^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{4}\kappa+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S306)

which captures the damping effects of the Hubbard bands.

Near the
\Gamma
point, we expand
\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n},1/\tau_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
in powers of
|{\mathbf{k}}|
. At each order, we obtain its coefficient in powers of
\kappa
. For
\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
we find

\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}\approx\frac{U_{1}}{2\sqrt{U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}}}\bigg[1-(N_{f}+1)\pi\kappa\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{4\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}}\log\bigg( \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2(U_1^2+4\gamma^2)}{U_1^2|v_\star\kk|^2}
\bigg)+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})\bigg]|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

(S307)

which described the renormalization of the velocity of the Dirac node.
In addition, the scattering rate reads

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}\approx\frac{(N_{f}+1)U_{1}\gamma^{2}\pi^{2}}{4(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2})^{3/2}}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|\kappa

(S308)

VI.1
Effect of finite
M

We next investigate the effect of finite
M
.
Similarly to the
M=0
limit, the self-energy of
f
-electron can be written as (
appendices

VI
and
S260
)

\displaystyle\Sigma_{f}^{CNP}(i\omega)=\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}}{4i(i\omega)\omega}

\displaystyle{Z_{M,a}(i\omega)=1-\pi(N_{f}+1)\kappa f_{M}(i\omega)=1-\pi(N_{f}+1)\kappa\bigg[\log\bigg( \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2+\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg)-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{M}{2i\omega}\log\bigg( \frac{i\omega+M}{i\omega-M}
\bigg)}-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\frac{M^2+\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg)\bigg]}

(S309)

Via the auxiliary field, the effective Green’s function is defined as

\displaystyle[\tilde{G}^{aux,\eta s}(\tau,{\mathbf{k}})]_{ij}=-\langle T_{\tau}[\tilde{\psi}_{{\mathbf{k}},\eta s}]_{i}(\tau)[\tilde{\psi}_{{\mathbf{k}},\eta s}^{\dagger}]_{j}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle\tilde{\psi}_{{\mathbf{k}},\eta s}=\begin{bmatrix}f_{{\mathbf{k}},1\eta s}&f_{{\mathbf{k}},2\eta s}&c_{{\mathbf{k}},1\eta s}&c_{{\mathbf{k}},2\eta s}&c_{{\mathbf{k}},3\eta s}&c_{{\mathbf{k}},4\eta s}&a_{{\mathbf{k}},1\eta s}&a_{{\mathbf{k}},2\eta s}\end{bmatrix}

(S310)

which takes the form of

\displaystyle[\tilde{G}^{aux,\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}i\omega&0&-\gamma&0&0&0&-\frac{U_{1}}{2}&0\\
0&i\omega&0&-\gamma&0&0&0&-\frac{U_{1}}{2}\\
-\gamma&0&i\omega&0&-v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})&0&0&0\\
0&-\gamma&0&i\omega&0&-v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})&0&0\\
0&0&-v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})&0&i\omega&-M&0&0\\
0&0&0&-v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})&-M&i\omega&0&0\\
-\frac{U_{1}}{2}&0&0&0&0&0&Z_{M,a}^{-1}(i\omega)i\omega&0\\
0&-\frac{U_{1}}{2}&0&0&0&0&0&Z_{M,a}^{-1}(i\omega)i\omega\end{bmatrix}

(S311)

We now consider the trace of Green’s function for the physical degrees of freedom

\displaystyle\mathcal{\tilde{G}}^{\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=\text{Tr}[\tilde{G}^{aux,\alpha\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})\tilde{P}_{phys}]

(S312)

where the projection matrix only keeps the physical degrees of freedom
\tilde{P}_{phys}=\text{diag}\{1,1,1,1,1,1,0,0\}
.
The spectral function of the system can be obtained from
\mathcal{\tilde{G}}
as

\displaystyle\tilde{\rho}^{\eta s}(\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=-\frac{1}{\pi}\mathrm{Im}\bigg[\mathcal{\tilde{G}}^{\eta s}(i\omega\rightarrow\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}})\bigg]

(S313)

Written explicitly, the trace of Green’s function takes the form of

\displaystyle\mathcal{\tilde{G}}^{\eta s}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=\frac{D_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}}}{\prod_{n=1}^{4}[(i\omega)^{2}-(\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})^{2}]}=\sum_{n=1}^{4}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{A}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}}{i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}}-\frac{\mathcal{A}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}}{i\omega+\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}}\bigg]

(S314)

with

\displaystyle D_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{i\omega}{8}\bigg[16\gamma^{4}(i\omega)^{2}+[4(i\omega)^{2}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)][4(3(i\omega)^{4}+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4}-2(i\omega)^{2}(M^{2}+2|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})+U_{1}^{2}(-2(i\omega)^{2}+M^{2}+2|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})Z_{M,a}(i\omega)]

\displaystyle+4\gamma^{2}[-16(i\omega)^{4}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)(M^{2}+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})+(i\omega)^{2}[8(M^{2}+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})+3U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)]]\bigg]

(S315)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}=\frac{D_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}}}{2\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}}\bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{4}\bigg[\prod_{m,m\neq n}[(i\omega)^{2}-(\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},m})^{2}]\bigg]\bigg)^{-1}

(S316)

We also note that, due to the particle-hole symmetry, we have contributions from both
1/(i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})
and
1/(i\omega+\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})
. It is sufficient to focus on one of them.
In addition,
\{\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})\}_{n=1,...,4}
corresponds to the solutions of the following equation with unknown variable
x

\displaystyle\bigg[x^{4}-x^{2}\bigg(\gamma^{2}+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}/4\bigg]^{2}-M^{2}x^{2}\bigg(x^{2}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-\gamma^{2}\bigg)^{2}=0

(S317)

which is derived from the determinant of the matrix in
section

VI.1
(see also the the discussion near
eq.

S287
).
Factorization of
eq.

S317
gives the following two equations

\displaystyle x^{4}-x^{2}\bigg(\gamma^{2}+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}/4+Mx\bigg(x^{2}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-\gamma^{2}\bigg)=0

(S318)

\displaystyle x^{4}-x^{2}\bigg(\gamma^{2}+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}/4-Mx\bigg(x^{2}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-\gamma^{2}\bigg)=0

(S319)

Assume
\{\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})\}_{n=1,...,4}
are the solutions of
eq.

S318
.
Then
\{-\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})\}_{n=1,...,4}
will be solutions of
eq.

S319
.
Thus we only need to solve
eq.

S318
.

We focus on the behaviors near the
\Gamma
point.
Writing the solution as
x^{*}=x_{0}^{*}+x_{1}^{*}
where
x_{0}^{*}
and
x_{1}^{*}
are zeroth and first order terms of
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}
.
Substituting
x=x_{0}^{*}+x_{1}^{*}
into
eq.

S318
and keep up to the first order of
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}
we have

\displaystyle[(x_{0}^{*})^{4}+4(x_{0}^{*})^{3}x_{1}^{*}]-[(x_{0}^{*})^{2}+2x_{0}^{*}x_{1}^{*}]\bigg(\gamma^{2}+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}/4

\displaystyle+M(x_{0}^{*}+x_{1}^{*})\bigg([(x_{0}^{*})^{2}+2x_{0}^{*}x_{1}^{*}]-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-\gamma^{2}\bigg)+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})=0

(S320)

The equation for
x_{0}^{*}
is

\displaystyle(x_{0}^{*})^{4}-(x_{0}^{*})^{2}\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)+Mx_{0}^{*}\bigg((x_{0}^{*})^{2}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-\gamma^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle x_{0}^{*}\bigg[(x_{0}^{*})^{3}+M\bigg((x_{0}^{*})^{2}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-\gamma^{2}\bigg)-x_{0}^{*}\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle x_{0}^{*}\bigg[(x_{0}^{*})^{2}(x_{0}^{*}+M)-(M+x_{0}^{*})\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle x_{0}^{*}(x_{0}^{*}+M)\bigg(x_{0}^{*}-\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}\bigg)\bigg(x_{0}^{*}+\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}\bigg)=0

(S321)

which gives
x_{0}^{*}=0,-M,\pm\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}
.
The equation for
x_{1}^{*}
is

\displaystyle 4(x_{0}^{*})^{3}x_{1}^{*}-(x_{0}^{*})^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}-2x_{0}^{*}x_{1}^{*}\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}/4

\displaystyle+Mx_{1}^{*}\bigg((x_{0}^{*})^{2}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-\gamma^{2}\bigg)+2M(x_{0}^{*})^{2}x_{1}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle x_{1}^{*}\bigg[4(x_{0}^{*})^{3}+3M(x_{0}^{*})^{2}-(2x_{0}^{*}+M)\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)\bigg]-(x_{0}^{*})^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}/4=0

(S322)

which gives

x_{1}^{*}=\frac{(x_{0}^{*})^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}/4}{4(x_{0}^{*})^{3}+3M(x_{0}^{*})^{2}-(2x_{0}^{*}+M)\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)}

(S323)

Thus, for the four solutions
x_{0}^{*}=0,-M,\pm\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}
, the corresponding first order corrections with respect to
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}
are

\displaystyle x_{1}^{*}\bigg|_{x_{0}^{*}=0}=

\displaystyle\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}/4}{M\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)}

\displaystyle x_{1}^{*}\bigg|_{x_{0}^{*}=-M}=

\displaystyle\frac{M^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}-U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}/4}{-M^{3}+M\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)}=-\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-M^{2}}{M\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-M^{2}\bigg)}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}

\displaystyle x_{1}^{*}\bigg|_{x_{0}^{*}=\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}}=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{2\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)\bigg(M+\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}\bigg)}

\displaystyle x_{1}^{*}\bigg|_{x_{0}^{*}=-\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}}=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{2\bigg(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4\bigg)\bigg(M-\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}\bigg)}

(S324)

In summary, by treating
{\mathbf{k}}
as a small parameter,
we obtain the following roots

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=1}\approx\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}{M[\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4]}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=2}\approx-M-\frac{U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4-M^{2}}{M\bigg[U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2}\bigg]}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=3}\approx\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}+\frac{\gamma^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{2(U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4+\gamma^{2})\bigg(\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}+M\bigg)}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=4}\approx-\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}-\frac{\gamma^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{2(U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4+\gamma^{2})\bigg(\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}Z_{M,a}(i\omega)/4}-M\bigg)}

(S325)

In the Hubbard-I limit where
Z_{M,a}(i\omega)=1
, we obtain the following dispersion for each valley and spin sector

\displaystyle E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=1}\bigg|_{Z_{M,a}(i\omega)=1}\approx\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4}{M[\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4]}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}

\displaystyle E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=2}\bigg|_{Z_{M,a}(i\omega)=1}\approx-M-\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4-M^{2}}{M\bigg[\bigg(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}\bigg)-M^{2}\bigg]}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}

\displaystyle E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=3}\bigg|_{Z_{M,a}(i\omega)=1}\approx\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}+\frac{\gamma^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{2(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2})\bigg(\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}+M\bigg)}

\displaystyle E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=4}\bigg|_{Z_{M,a}(i\omega)=1}\approx-\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}-\frac{\gamma^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{2(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2})\bigg(\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}-M\bigg)}

(S326)

Again, due to the particle-hole symmetry, we also have the excitation mode with dispersion
-\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}|_{Z_{M,a}(i\omega)=1}=-E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
.

We now focus on the low-energy gapless excitation described by
\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=1}
, which leads to a quadratic gapless excitation in the Hubbard-I limit. We now investigate the effect of self-energy at order of
\kappa
. Similarly to
eq.

S294
, we consider its real frequency behaviors and let

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{M}_{\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}},1}=\mathcal{R}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}-i{\Gamma}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}

(S327)

such that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega-{\mathcal{E}}^{M}_{\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}},1}}=\frac{1}{\omega-{\mathcal{R}}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}+i{{\Gamma}}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}}

(S328)

From
sections

VI.1
,
VI.1
and
IV.4
, up to first order of
\kappa
we find

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}\approx

\displaystyle\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4}{M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)}\bigg\{1-\frac{\gamma^{2}\pi}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}(N_{f}+1)\kappa\bigg[\log\bigg( \bigg| \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 -\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{M}{2\omega}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M+\omega}{M-\omega}\bigg|\bigg)

\displaystyle{-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2-\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})\bigg\}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}

(S329)

and

\displaystyle{\Gamma}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}=\frac{\gamma^{2}U_{1}^{2}\pi}{4M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)^{2}}\kappa(N_{f}+1)\text{sgn}(\omega)\bigg[\pi\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\omega|)+\frac{\pi}{2}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}-|\omega|}{|\omega|}\theta(M-|\omega|)+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})\bigg]|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}

(S330)

Denote the renormalized dispersion as
\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
.
Then the renormalized dispersion of the gapless mode
(n=1)
is determined by

\displaystyle(\omega-{\mathcal{R}}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1})\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}=0

(S331)

Assume
\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}
could be written as a series of
\kappa
:

\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+\tilde{E}^{M,(1)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S332)

where the superscript
(i)
means the
i
-th order of
\kappa
.
Then, expand
eq.

S331
to first order of
\kappa
we find

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+\tilde{E}^{M,(1)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}+(\partial_{\omega}{\mathcal{R}}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1})\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}\times\tilde{E}^{M,(1)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)}\bigg\{1-\frac{\gamma^{2}\pi}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}(N_{f}+1)\kappa\bigg[\log\bigg( \bigg| \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 -\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{M}{2\omega}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M+\omega}{M-\omega}\bigg|\bigg)

\displaystyle{-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2-\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)}\bigg]\bigg\}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}

\displaystyle-\kappa\tilde{E}^{M,(1)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\frac{2\gamma^{2}\pi(N_{f}+1)U_{1}^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M(U_{1}^{2}+4\gamma^{2})^{2}\omega^{2}(-|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+\omega^{2})}\bigg[4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}\omega+M(-|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+\omega^{2})\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M+\omega}{M-\omega}\bigg|\bigg)\bigg]\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)}\bigg\{1-\frac{\gamma^{2}\pi}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}(N_{f}+1)\kappa\bigg[\log\bigg( \bigg| \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 -\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{M}{2\omega}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M+\omega}{M-\omega}\bigg|\bigg)

\displaystyle{-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2-\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)}\bigg]\bigg\}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S333)

Comparing the zeroth term of
\kappa
we find

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=

\displaystyle\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)}

(S334)

Substitute
eq.

S334
to
eq.

S333
we have

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+\tilde{E}^{M,(1)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\bigg\{1-\frac{\gamma^{2}\pi}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}(N_{f}+1)\kappa\bigg[\log\bigg( \bigg| \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 -(\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{\kk,1})^2}{(\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{\kk,1})^2}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{M}{2\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M+\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{\kk,1}}{M-\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{\kk,1}}\bigg|\bigg)

\displaystyle{-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2-(\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{\kk,1})^2}{(\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{\kk,1})^2}\bigg|\bigg)}\bigg]\bigg\}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S335)

Near
|{\mathbf{k}}|\to 0
, we have
\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\sim|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}
. Only keep up to the
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}
term,
eq.

S335
becomes

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\bigg\{1-\frac{\gamma^{2}\pi}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}(N_{f}+1)\kappa\bigg[\log\bigg( \bigg| \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 }{(\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{\kk,1})^2}\bigg|\bigg)-1{-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2}{(\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{\kk,1})^2}\bigg|\bigg)}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})\bigg\}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)}\bigg\{1-\frac{\gamma^{2}\pi}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}(N_{f}+1)\kappa\bigg[2\log\bigg( \frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|M(\gamma^2 +U_1^2/4)}{U_1^2/4|v_\star\kk|^2}\bigg)-1{-\log\bigg( \frac{M^2(\gamma^2 +U_1^2/4)}{U_1^2/4|v_\star\kk|^2}\bigg)}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)}\bigg\{1-\frac{\gamma^{2}\pi}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}(N_{f}+1)\kappa\bigg[\log\bigg( \frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2(4\gamma^2 +U_1^2)}{U_1^2|v_\star\kk|^2}\bigg)-1\bigg]\bigg\}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

(S336)

The expression for the scattering time
eq.

S330
is already first order term of
\kappa
.
Thus up to first order of
\kappa
, the scattering time in the limit of
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|\to 0
is

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\tau}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}=

\displaystyle{\Gamma}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}={\Gamma}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma^{2}U_{1}^{2}\pi|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{4M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)^{2}}\kappa(N_{f}+1)\mathrm{sgn}(\omega)\bigg(\pi+\frac{\pi}{2}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}-|\omega|}{|\omega|}\bigg)\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma^{2}U_{1}^{2}\pi^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{8M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)^{2}}\kappa(N_{f}+1)\mathrm{sgn}(\omega)+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{\gamma^{2}U_{1}^{2}\pi^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{8(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)^{2}}\kappa(N_{f}+1)\frac{1}{\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{M\gamma^{2}\pi^{2}}{2(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)}(N_{f}+1)\kappa}+\frac{\gamma^{2}U_{1}^{2}\pi^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{8M(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)^{2}}\kappa(N_{f}+1)\mathrm{sgn}(\omega)+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{M\gamma^{2}\pi^{2}}{2(\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4)}(N_{f}+1)\kappa}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

(S337)

Using the relation between
f_{M}(i\omega)
, hybridization function
\Delta_{M}(i\omega)
and
c
electron spectral function
\rho_{c}^{hyb}(\epsilon)

eqs.

S260
and
S258
, the scattering time could be expressed as (
eq.

S330
)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\tau}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}=

\displaystyle E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}{\pi\kappa\mathrm{Im}[f_{M}(\omega+i0^{+})]}\bigg|_{\omega=E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}\frac{\gamma^{2}\mathrm{Im}[\Delta_{M}(\omega+i0^{+})]}{\omega}\bigg|_{\omega=E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}\frac{\pi\rho_{c}^{hyb}(\omega)}{\omega}\bigg|_{\omega=E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}{\pi\rho_{c}^{hyb}(E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1})}

(S338)

which is proportional to the density of state of the quadratic gapless mode
E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}
(
section

VI.1
).
As the DOS of a quadratic band is finite, the scattering rate is also finite as given by
eq.

S337
.
As a comparison, for the
M=0
case, from
eq.

S292
, we can show that the scattering time of the gapless mode can also be written in a similar form as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\tau}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}=

\displaystyle E_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\frac{1}{2}\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}{\pi\kappa\mathrm{Im}[f(\omega+i0^{+})]}\bigg|_{\omega=E_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\gamma^{2}(N_{f}+1)}{\gamma^{2}+U_{1}^{2}/4}\pi\rho_{c}^{hyb}(E_{{\mathbf{k}},1})\bigg|_{M=0}

(S339)

which is proportional to the density of state linear dispersive gapless mode
E_{{\mathbf{k}},1}
.
Thus the scattering time will be linear in
|{\mathbf{k}}|
as given by
eq.

S308
.

Then we investigate the
\pm M
gapped excitations described by
\pm\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=2}
.
We write

\mathcal{E}^{M}_{\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}},2}=\mathcal{R}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}-i{\Gamma}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}

(S340)

Expanding
\mathcal{E}^{M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n=2}
in
section

VI.1
to the first order of
\kappa
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}\approx

\displaystyle E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\gamma^{2}}{M(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\pi(N_{f}+1)\kappa\mathrm{Re}[f_{M}(\omega+i0^{+})]+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

\displaystyle\Gamma^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}\approx

\displaystyle-\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\gamma^{2}}{M(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\pi(N_{f}+1)\kappa\mathrm{Im}[f_{M}(\omega+i0^{+})]+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S341)

The renormalized dispersion
\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}
is given by
