(\omega-\mathcal{R}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2})\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}}=0

(S342)

Write
\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}
into series of
\kappa
as

\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+\tilde{E}^{M,(1)}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S343)

Then, similar to the case of the gapless excitation, we have

\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=-M-\frac{(U_{1}^{2}/4-M^{2})|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})}

(S344)

In addition, the renormalized dispersion and scattering time (up to first order of
\kappa
) are

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}}=E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\gamma^{2}}{M(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\pi(N_{f}+1)\kappa\mathrm{Re}[f_{M}(E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})]+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\tau}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}=

\displaystyle\Gamma^{M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{M,(0)}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}}=-\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\gamma^{2}}{M(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\pi(N_{f}+1)\kappa\mathrm{Im}[f_{M}(E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})]+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2})

(S345)

Near
|{\mathbf{k}}|\to 0
,
E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=-M+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})
.
Besides, as
M<U_{1}/2
, we always have
E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}<-M
and
|E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}|>M
.
Thus, keep up to the lowest order of
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}
term,
f_{M}(E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})
is evaluated as

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}[f_{M}(E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})]=

\displaystyle\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-(E^{M}_{\kk,2})^2}{(E^{M}_{\kk,2})^2}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{M}{2E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{E^{M}_{\kk,2}+M}{E^{M}_{\kk,2}-M}\bigg|\bigg)-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2-(E^{M}_{\kk,2})^2}{(E^{M}_{\kk,2})^2}\bigg|\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log[\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}-M^{2}}{M^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})\bigg]+\frac{1}{2}\log[\frac{(U_{1}^{2}/4-M^{2})|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{2M^{2}(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\log[\frac{2(U_{1}^{2}/4-M^{2})|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M^{2}(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-M^2}{2M^2}\bigg)+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

\displaystyle\mathrm{Im}[f_{M}(E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})]=

\displaystyle\pi\mathrm{sgn}(E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2})\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}|)+\frac{\pi}{2}\frac{M-|E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}|}{E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}}\theta(M-|E^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}|)=-\pi

(S346)

Then the renormalized dispersion and scattering time become

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=

\displaystyle-M-\frac{(U_{1}^{2}/4-M^{2})|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})}+\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\gamma^{2}}{M(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\pi(N_{f}+1)\kappa\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-M^2}{2M^2}\bigg)+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tilde{\tau}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{U_{1}^{2}/4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}\gamma^{2}}{M(U_{1}^{2}/4+\gamma^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\pi^{2}(N_{f}+1)\kappa+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

(S347)

Appendix VII
c
-electron self-energy from
fc
interactions at charge neutrality
\nu=0

In Appendix III of the SM
179
to
appendix

VI
,
we focused on correlation effects arising from the
Hubbard interaction
U_{1}
, treating the hybridization
\gamma
perturbatively
and retaining contributions up to order
\gamma^{2}
.
This correlation effects first generate a dynamical self-energy for the
f
electrons, which subsequently contribute to the damping rate of the hybridized bands.

In this section, we turn to correlation effects induced by the
fc
interactions

H_{J}
and
H_{W}
, which will also generate a dynamical self-energy and finite scattering rate.
We begin in the zero-hybridization limit, where the
fc

interactions are treated at the Hartree-Fock level previously.
(Although when calculating the hybridization function, we take the approximation
W_{1}=W_{2}
and
J=0
to get an analytic formula. While in the other parts, the HF contribution of
H_{J}
is kept.)
We then incorporate
dynamical correlation effects generated by the
fc
interactions by expanding
the self-energy to second order in the
fc
interaction strength, with the first-order term corresponding to the Hartree-Fock contribution.
Throughout this section, we set
\gamma=0
and focus on charge neutrality to simplify the analysis. Since, in the
\gamma=0
limit, the low-energy gapless excitations are formed by the
c
electrons, we focus on the self-energy of the
c
electrons.

We consider a generic
fc
interaction taking the form of

\displaystyle H_{fc}^{int}=\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},{\mathbf{k}},{\mathbf{q}}}\sum_{iji^{\prime}j^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{iji^{\prime}j^{\prime}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}c_{{\mathbf{k}},i^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{{\mathbf{k}}+{\mathbf{q}},j^{\prime}}e^{i{\mathbf{q}}\cdot{\mathbf{R}}}

(S348)

To simplify the notation, we use index
i
(also
j,i^{\prime},j^{\prime}
) to characterize the unified spin, orbital, and valley degrees of freedom.
\mathcal{J}_{iji^{\prime}j^{\prime}}
denotes a generic
fc
interactions including both
H_{W}
and
H_{J}
.
Written explicitly, via the original spin, valley, and orbital indices, the interaction tensor takes the form

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\alpha_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},\alpha_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}=W\delta_{\alpha_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},\alpha_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\delta_{a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}-\frac{J}{2}\delta_{\alpha_{1}+2,a_{4}}\delta_{\alpha_{2}+2,a_{3}}\delta_{\eta_{1}s_{1},\eta_{4}s_{4}}\delta_{\eta_{2}s_{2},\eta_{3}s_{3}}(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+(-1)^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}})

(S349)

where we also assume
W_{1}=W_{3}=W
for simplicity.

We aim to calculate the effect of
H_{fc}^{int}
on the
c
electron self-energy. We separate the system into

\displaystyle S=S_{decouple}+S_{fc}

\displaystyle S_{decouple}=\int_{\tau}\sum_{\eta sa,{\mathbf{k}}}c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}},a\eta s}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}c_{{\mathbf{k}},a\eta s}(\tau)+\int_{\tau}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}(\tau)+\int_{\tau}[H^{\prime}(\tau)|_{H^{(cf,\eta)}=0}]

\displaystyle S_{fc}=\int_{\tau}[H_{fc}^{int}(\tau)-H_{fc}^{HF}(\tau)]

(S350)

where
H^{\prime}
is the meanfield Hamiltonian given by (
appendices

III
and
III
)

\displaystyle H^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta s}\sum_{aa^{\prime}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}(H^{(c,\eta)}_{a,a^{\prime}}({\mathbf{k}}+{\mathbf{G}}))c_{{\mathbf{k}},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{{\mathbf{k}},a^{\prime}\eta s}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N_{M}}}\sum_{\eta s\alpha s}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\bigg(e^{-i{\mathbf{k}}\cdot{\mathbf{R}}}H_{a\alpha}^{(cf,\eta)}({\mathbf{k}})c_{{\mathbf{k}},a\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}+h.c.\bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}(\epsilon_{f}-\mu)f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}+{\frac{U_{1}}{2}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s\neq\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}}\,.

(S351)

\displaystyle H^{(c,\eta)}({\mathbf{k}})=\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{0}(\epsilon_{c,1}-\mu)&v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}+ik_{y}\sigma_{z})\\
v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}-ik_{y}\sigma_{z})&\sigma_{0}(\epsilon_{c,2}-\mu)+M\sigma_{x}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle H^{(cf,\eta)}({\mathbf{k}})=\begin{pmatrix}\gamma\sigma_{0}+v_{\star}^{\prime}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{x}+k_{y}\sigma_{y})\\
0_{2\times 2}\end{pmatrix}

(S352)

which includes the non-interacting Hamiltonian
H_{0}
, the Hubbard interactions
H_{U_{1}}
, Hartree-Fock contributions from the
H_{V}
and the Hartree-Fock contributions from
H_{fc}^{int}
denoted by
H_{fc}^{HF}
.
By taking the single particle
cf
hybridization strength to be zero
H^{(cf,\eta)}=0
, the
c
and
f
electrons are decoupled and could be separated into

\displaystyle S_{decouple}=

\displaystyle S_{c}+S_{atom}

\displaystyle S_{c}=

\displaystyle\int_{\tau}\sum_{\eta sa,{\mathbf{k}}}c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}},a\eta s}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}c_{{\mathbf{k}},a\eta s}(\tau)+\int_{\tau}H_{c}(\tau)

\displaystyle S_{atom}=

\displaystyle\int_{\tau}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}(\tau)+\int_{\tau}H_{atom}(\tau)

\displaystyle H_{c}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta s}\sum_{aa^{\prime}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}(H^{(c,\eta)}_{a,a^{\prime}}({\mathbf{k}}+{\mathbf{G}}))c_{{\mathbf{k}},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{{\mathbf{k}},a^{\prime}\eta s}

\displaystyle H_{atom}=

\displaystyle\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}(\epsilon_{f}-\mu)f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}+{\frac{U_{1}}{2}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s\neq\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}}

(S353)

Note that
H_{atom}
is nothing but the atomic Hamiltonian discussed defined in
eqs.

S69
and
S110
.
In addition,
S_{fc}
characterizes the interaction effect induced by
H_{fc}^{int}
that goes beyond the Hartree-Fock approximation.
Written explicitly, we have

\displaystyle[H_{fc}^{int}-H_{fc}^{HF}]=\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},{\mathbf{k}},{\mathbf{q}}}\sum_{iji^{\prime}j^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{iji^{\prime}j^{\prime}}:f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}::c_{{\mathbf{k}},i^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{{\mathbf{k}}+{\mathbf{q}},j^{\prime}}:e^{i{\mathbf{q}}\cdot{\mathbf{R}}}

(S354)

where we have defined the normal order with respect to the ground state of
S_{decouple}
as

\displaystyle:f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}:=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}-\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

\displaystyle:c_{{\mathbf{k}},i^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{{\mathbf{k}}+{\mathbf{q}},j^{\prime}}:=c_{{\mathbf{k}},i^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{{\mathbf{k}}+{\mathbf{q}},j^{\prime}}-\delta_{{\mathbf{q}},0}\langle c_{{\mathbf{k}},i^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{{\mathbf{k}},j^{\prime}}\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

(S355)

where,
\langle O\rangle_{S_{decouple}}
is defined as

\displaystyle\langle O\rangle_{S_{decouple}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{S_{decouple}}}\int D[f,f^{\dagger},c,c^{\dagger}]Oe^{-S_{decouple}}

\displaystyle Z_{S_{decouple}}=

\displaystyle\int D[f,f^{\dagger},c,c^{\dagger}]e^{-S_{decouple}}

(S356)

For operators
O_{c}
constructed by
c
electrons and
O_{c}
constructed by
f
, we have

\displaystyle\langle O_{c}O_{f}\rangle_{S_{decouple}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{S_{decouple}}}\int D[f,f^{\dagger},c,c^{\dagger}]O_{c}O_{f}e^{-S_{c}-S_{atom}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{S_{decouple}}}\bigg[\int D[f,f^{\dagger}]O_{f}e^{-S_{atom}}\bigg]\bigg[\int D[c,c^{\dagger}]O_{c}e^{-S_{c}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{S_{decouple}}^{2}}\bigg[\int D[f,f^{\dagger},c,c^{\dagger}]O_{f}e^{-S_{decouple}}\bigg]\bigg[\int D[f,f^{\dagger},c,c^{\dagger}]O_{c}e^{-S_{decouple}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle O_{c}\rangle_{S_{decouple}}\langle O_{f}\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

(S357)

Besides, by integrating out the
c
electron,
\langle O_{f}\rangle_{S_{decouple}}
can also be written as
\langle O_{f}\rangle_{\gamma=0}
defined in
eq.

S72
.

We now aim to calculate the
c
-electron Green’s function by treating
\mathcal{J}_{iji^{\prime}j^{\prime}}
perturbatively. The Green’s function of
c
electron can now be calculated via

\displaystyle G_{c,ij}(\tau-\tau^{\prime},{\mathbf{k}})=-\langle T_{\tau}c_{{\mathbf{k}},i}(\tau)c_{{\mathbf{k}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau^{\prime})\rangle

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle-\langle T_{\tau}c_{{\mathbf{k}},i}(\tau)c_{{\mathbf{k}},j}(\tau^{\prime})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{M}}\int_{\tau_{1}}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},,{\mathbf{k}},{\mathbf{q}}}\sum_{iji^{\prime}j^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{iji^{\prime}j^{\prime}}e^{i{\mathbf{q}}\cdot{\mathbf{R}}}\langle T_{\tau}:f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{1})::c_{{\mathbf{k}},i^{\prime}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})c_{{\mathbf{k}}+{\mathbf{q}},j^{\prime}}(\tau_{1}):c_{{\mathbf{k}},i}(\tau)c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}},j}(\tau^{\prime})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2N^{2}}\int_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1},{\mathbf{R}}_{2},{\mathbf{k}}_{1},{\mathbf{q}}_{1},{\mathbf{k}}_{2},{\mathbf{q}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}e^{i{\mathbf{q}}_{1}\cdot{\mathbf{R}}_{1}+i{\mathbf{q}}_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{R}}_{2}}

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}:f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})::c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}}_{1},i_{1}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})c_{{\mathbf{k}}_{1}+{\mathbf{q}}_{1},j_{1}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1}):

\displaystyle:f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{2},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{2},j_{2}}(\tau_{2})::c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}}_{2},i_{2}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2})c_{{\mathbf{k}}_{2}+{\mathbf{q}}_{2},j_{2}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2}):c_{{\mathbf{k}},i}(\tau)c_{{\mathbf{k}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau^{\prime})\rangle_{S_{decouple},connect}

(S358)

where the subscript “connect” indicates that only the connected contribution is included.
According to
eq.

S357
, the first order term in
\mathcal{J}
is proportional to

\langle:f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{1}):\rangle_{S_{decouple}}=\langle f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{1})-\langle f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}(\tau_{1})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}\rangle_{S_{decouple}}=0

(S359)

Thus, the first order term vanishes.
As for the second order term, using Wick’s theorem we find

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}:f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})::c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}}_{1},i_{1}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})c_{{\mathbf{k}}_{1}+{\mathbf{q}}_{1},j_{1}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1}):

\displaystyle:f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{2},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{2},j_{2}}(\tau_{2})::c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}}_{2},i_{2}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2})c_{{\mathbf{k}}_{2}+{\mathbf{q}}_{2},j_{2}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2}):c_{{\mathbf{k}},i}(\tau)c_{{\mathbf{k}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau^{\prime})\rangle_{S_{decouple},connect}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}:f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})::f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{2},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}}_{2},j_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{S_{decouple}}\times

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}:c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}}_{1},i_{1}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})c_{{\mathbf{k}}_{1}+{\mathbf{q}}_{1},j_{1}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})::c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}}_{2},i_{2}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2})c_{{\mathbf{k}}_{2}+{\mathbf{q}}_{2},j_{2}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2}):c_{{\mathbf{k}},i}(\tau)c_{{\mathbf{k}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau^{\prime})\rangle_{S_{decouple},connect}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1},{\mathbf{R}}_{2}}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})\langle T_{\tau}c_{{\mathbf{k}},i}(\tau):c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}}_{1},i_{1}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})c_{{\mathbf{k}}_{1}+{\mathbf{q}}_{1},j_{1}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})::c^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{k}}_{2},i_{2}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2})c_{{\mathbf{k}}_{2}+{\mathbf{q}}_{2},j_{2}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2}):c_{{\mathbf{k}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau^{\prime})\rangle_{S_{decouple},connect}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\delta_{{\mathbf{R}}_{1},{\mathbf{R}}_{2}}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})\delta_{{\mathbf{k}},{\mathbf{k}}_{1}}G^{S_{decouple}}_{c,ii_{1}^{\prime}}(\tau-\tau_{1},{\mathbf{k}})\delta_{{\mathbf{k}}_{1}+{\mathbf{q}}_{1},{\mathbf{k}}_{2}}G_{c,j_{1}^{\prime}i_{2}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},{\mathbf{k}}_{2})\delta_{{\mathbf{k}}_{2}+{\mathbf{q}}_{2},{\mathbf{k}}}G_{c,j_{2}^{\prime}j}^{S_{decouple}}(\tau_{2}-\tau^{\prime},{\mathbf{k}})

(S360)

where

\displaystyle G_{c,ij}^{S_{decouple}}(\tau-\tau^{\prime},{\mathbf{k}})=-\langle T_{\tau}c_{{\mathbf{k}},i}(\tau)c_{{\mathbf{k}},j}^{\dagger}(\tau^{\prime})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

\displaystyle\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})=\langle T_{\tau}:f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})::f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

(S361)

denotes the Hartree-Fock Green’s function of
c
electrons and the susceptibility (four-fermion correlation) function of
f
electrons at the zero-hybridization limit.
In summary, up to second order of
\mathcal{J}
, the
c
electron Green’s is written as

\displaystyle G_{c,ij}(\tau-\tau^{\prime},{\mathbf{k}})

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle G_{c,ij}^{S_{decouple}}(\tau-\tau^{\prime},{\mathbf{k}}){+}\frac{1}{N^{2}}\int_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},{\mathbf{q}}}\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})G_{c,j_{1}^{\prime}i_{2}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},{\mathbf{k}}-{\mathbf{q}})G_{c,j_{2}^{\prime}j}^{S_{decouple}}(\tau_{2}-\tau^{\prime},{\mathbf{k}})G_{c,ii_{1}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}(\tau-\tau_{1},{\mathbf{k}})

(S362)

In the Matsubara frequency domain, we find

\displaystyle G_{c,ij}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\tau,\tau^{\prime}}G_{c,ij}(\tau-\tau^{\prime},{\mathbf{k}})e^{i\omega(\tau-\tau^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle G_{c,ij}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})+\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\sum_{i\Omega}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega)\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime}}G_{c,j_{1}^{\prime}i_{2}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}({\mathbf{k}}^{\prime},i\omega-i\Omega)G_{c,j_{2}^{\prime}j}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})G_{c,ii_{1}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})

(S363)

where
\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega)=\int_{\tau}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(\tau)e^{i\Omega\tau}
.
In addition, in the zero hybridization limit and exact integer filling we have
\epsilon_{c,1}=\epsilon_{c,2}=\mu
with
c
electron filling
\nu_{c}=0
(see discussion in
section

IV.4
).
Therefore, the Hartree Fock Green’s function of
c
electron is written as

\displaystyle G^{S_{decouple}}_{c,a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=

\displaystyle\delta_{\eta_{1}s_{1},\eta_{2}s_{2}}\bigg[i\omega\mathbb{I}-H^{(c,\eta_{1})}({\mathbf{k}})\bigg]^{-1}_{a_{1},a_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{s_{1},s_{2}}\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{2}}\bigg[i\omega\mathbb{I}-\begin{pmatrix}0_{2\times 2}&v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}+ik_{y}\sigma_{z})\\
v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}-ik_{y}\sigma_{z})&M\sigma_{x}\end{pmatrix}\bigg]^{-1}_{a_{1},a_{2}}

(S364)

We can also use Dyson’s equation to obtain the self-energy of
c
electrons at order
\mathcal{J}^{2}
. In the matrix form, we define the self-energy as

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=[G_{c}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1}-[G_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1}

(S365)

which indicates

\displaystyle G_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})\approx G_{c}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})+G_{c}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})\cdot\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})\cdot G_{c}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})+...

(S366)

Combining
appendices

VII
,
S365
and
S366
, we find

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{i_{1}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}\approx

\displaystyle{+}\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}j_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\beta}\sum_{i\Omega}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega)\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime}}G_{c,j_{1}^{\prime}i_{2}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega-i\Omega,{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime})+O(\mathcal{J}^{3})

(S367)

VII.1
Evaluation of
\Sigma_{c}(i\omega)

We now evaluate
\Sigma_{c}
explicitly. We focus on charge neutrality with
f
electron filling
n_{f}=N_{f}/2
.

We first consider the susceptibility of
f
electron (
appendix

VII
).
At charge neutrality, we have

\displaystyle:f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}:=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}-\langle f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}\rangle_{S_{decouple}}=f_{{\mathbf{R}},i}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j}-\delta_{i,j}\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}

(S368)

Using
eq.

S145
, the four Fermion correlation function without normal order is calculated as (in the previous discussion of
\gamma
, the four Fermion correlation function
section

IV.2
is evaluated at four different time points, while in this section, the four Fermion correlation function is evaluated at two different time points)

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}=\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{\gamma=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}e^{-(\beta-\tau_{1}+\tau_{2})E_{n}-(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})E_{m}}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}|n\rangle&\tau_{1}>\tau_{2}\\
\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}e^{-(\beta-\tau_{2}+\tau_{1})E_{m}-(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})E_{n}}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}|n\rangle&\tau_{1}<\tau_{2}\\
\end{cases}

(S369)

where
|n\rangle,|m\rangle
denote the eigen states of atomic Hamiltonian of
f
electron with energy
E_{n},E_{m}
respectively (
eq.

S110
). We note that if the filling of the state
|n\rangle
is
n_{f}
, then the condition
\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}|n\rangle\neq 0
implies that the filling of the state
|m\rangle
is also
n_{f}
and that
E_{n}=E_{m}
.
Thus

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}\sum_{n,m}e^{-\beta E_{n}}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}|n\rangle

(S370)

We further note that

•

If
i_{1}=j_{1}
, the non-zero contributions are from the term with
\ket{m}=\ket{n}
, thus the form factor becomes

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\langle n|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}|m\rangle\langle m|f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}|n\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}\ket{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}\ket{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}+\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(1-\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}\ket{n}

(S371)

•

If
i_{1}\neq j_{1}
, the non-zero contributions come from the term with
\ket{m}=f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}
, then the form factors becomes

\displaystyle(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}\ket{m}\bra{m}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}\ket{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}\ket{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}\ket{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\delta_{i_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{1}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}\ket{n}

(S372)

In addition, in the low-temperature limit, we have the following relation (
eq.

S121
)

\lim_{\beta\to+\infty}\frac{e^{-\beta E_{n}}}{Z_{atom,{\mathbf{R}}}}=\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}^{-1}\delta_{n,gs}

(S373)

Therefore, in the low-temperature limit,
eq.

S370
becomes

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}^{-1}\sum_{n\in GS}\bigg[\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}+\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(1-\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}})\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}\ket{n}

\displaystyle+(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\delta_{i_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{1}}\bra{n}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}\ket{n}\bra{n}f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}\ket{n}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}}\frac{\binom{N_{f}-1}{n_{f}-1}}{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}+\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(1-\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}})\frac{\binom{N_{f}-2}{n_{f}-2}}{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}+(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\delta_{i_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{1}}\frac{\binom{N_{f}-2}{n_{f}-1}}{\binom{N_{f}}{n_{f}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}}\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}+\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(1-\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}})\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}+(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\delta_{i_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{1}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}

(S374)

Then the susceptibility takes the form of

\displaystyle\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}:f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})::f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}

\displaystyle-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}\langle f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{2}}(\tau_{2})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{2}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}-\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}\langle f^{\dagger}_{{\mathbf{R}},i_{1}}(\tau_{1})f_{{\mathbf{R}},j_{1}}(\tau_{1})\rangle_{S_{decouple}}+\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\frac{n_{f}^{2}}{N_{f}^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}}\frac{n_{f}}{N_{f}}+\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(1-\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}})\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-1)}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}+(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\delta_{i_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{1}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\frac{n_{f}^{2}}{N_{f}^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}+\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(1-\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}})\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}+(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\delta_{i_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{1}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}

(S375)

In the frequency domain, we find

\displaystyle\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta\delta_{\Omega,0}\bigg[\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}+\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(1-\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}})\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}+(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\delta_{i_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{1}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

(S376)

We first prove that
H_{W}
do not contribute to
\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})
in the zero-temperature limit.
From
section

VII.1
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}}\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\frac{1}{\beta}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega=0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}}\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\bigg[\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}+\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(1-\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}})\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}+(1-\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}})\delta_{i_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{1}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}}\bigg[\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}+\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(1-\delta_{i_{1},i_{2}})\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}+\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}(N_{f}-1)\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}=0

(S377)

Similarly, we also have

\sum_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\frac{1}{\beta}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega=0)=0

(S378)

Denote (
eq.

S349
)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{i}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{W}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{i}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}+\mathcal{J}^{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{i}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{i}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}^{W}=

\displaystyle W\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{1}^{\prime},j_{1}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{J}_{\alpha_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},\alpha_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}=

\displaystyle-\frac{J}{2}\delta_{\alpha_{1}+2,a_{4}}\delta_{\alpha_{2}+2,a_{3}}\delta_{\eta_{1}s_{1},\eta_{4}s_{4}}\delta_{\eta_{2}s_{2},\eta_{3}s_{3}}(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+(-1)^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}})

(S379)

The self-energy of
c
electron now reads (from
eq.

S367
)

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{i_{1}^{\prime},j_{2}^{\prime}}\approx

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}j_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}}[W\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\delta_{i_{1}^{\prime},j_{1}^{\prime}}+\mathcal{J}^{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}]\mathcal{J}_{i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\beta}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega=0)\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime}}G_{c,j_{1}^{\prime}i_{2}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W\bigg[\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}}\delta_{i_{1},j_{1}}\frac{1}{\beta}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega=0)\bigg]\sum_{j_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}}\delta_{i_{1}^{\prime},j_{1}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}_{i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime}}G_{c,j_{1}^{\prime}i_{2}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}j_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}^{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}[W\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{i_{2}^{\prime},j_{2}^{\prime}}+\mathcal{J}^{J}_{i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}]\frac{1}{\beta}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega=0)\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime}}G_{c,j_{1}^{\prime}i_{2}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}j_{1}j_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}^{J}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}\mathcal{J}^{J}_{i_{2}j_{2}i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\beta}\chi_{i_{1}j_{1},i_{2}j_{2}}(i\Omega=0)\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime}}G_{c,j_{1}^{\prime}i_{2}^{\prime}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime})

(S380)

We can see that only
H_{J}
term contributes to the dynamical self-energy at low temperature through the
f
electron local-moment fluctuations.
On the other hand, as the charge fluctuations of
f
electron induced by
H_{W}
are frozen at low temperature,
H_{W}
term does not contribute any self-energy at second order.
The self-energy contributed by
H_{J}
is

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{+}J^{2}[\delta_{a_{1},3}+\delta_{a_{1},4}][\delta_{a_{2},3}+\delta_{a_{2},4}]\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime}}\sum_{\eta_{3}s_{3},\eta_{4}s_{4}}\sum_{a_{3},a_{4}\in\{3,4\}}G_{c,a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime})

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\delta_{a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}\delta_{a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}\delta_{a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(1-\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}})

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}[\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{3}}\delta_{a_{1},a_{3}}+\delta_{\eta_{1},-\eta_{3}}(1-\delta_{a_{1},a_{3}})]\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\delta_{a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(1-\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}})\bigg]

(S381)

We can observe that only
\Gamma_{1}\oplus\Gamma_{2}
electrons acquire a self-energy contribution coming from the
H_{J}
term.

VII.2
Evaluation of the Green’s function

We now evaluate the Green’s function of
c
electron at charge neutrality.
Using the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the
c
electron Hamiltonian
eqs.

S252
and
S253
, the
c
electron Green’s function
eq.

S364
is expressed as

\displaystyle G_{c,a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=

\displaystyle\delta_{s_{1},s_{2}}\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{2}}\sum_{n=1}^{4}\frac{U^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},a_{1}n}[U^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},a_{2}n}]^{*}}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}

(S382)

From
eq.

S381
, the self-energy only depends on the Green’s function of
\Gamma_{1}\oplus\Gamma_{2}
electrons with
a=3,4
.
We focus on the
\Gamma_{1}\oplus\Gamma_{2}
electrons and introduce the orbital diagonal and off-diagonal local Green’s functions as

\displaystyle g_{c}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}G_{c,3\eta_{1}s_{1},3\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}G_{c,4\eta_{1}s_{1},4\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\bigg[(A_{1,{\mathbf{k}}})^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}}\bigg)+(A_{2,{\mathbf{k}}})^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\bigg[\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}(\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}-M)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}(\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}+M)}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}}\bigg)\ \bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\frac{1}{4}\bigg[\frac{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}+M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}-M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}}\bigg)\ \bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\frac{1}{4}\bigg[\bigg(1+\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}}\bigg)\ \bigg]

\displaystyle g_{c,off}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}G_{c,3\eta_{1}s_{1},4\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})=\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}G_{c,4\eta_{1}s_{1},3\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\bigg[(A_{1,{\mathbf{k}}})^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4}\bigg(-\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}}\bigg)+(A_{2,{\mathbf{k}}})^{2}|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}-\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{{\mathbf{k}}}\frac{1}{4}\bigg[\bigg(1+\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg(-\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}}+\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}}-\frac{1}{i\omega-E^{\eta_{1},c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}}\bigg)\ \bigg]

(S383)

Combining
eqs.

S381
,
S382
and
S383
, we find

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{2}(\delta_{a_{1},3}+\delta_{a_{1},4})(\delta_{a_{2},3}+\delta_{a_{2},4})\sum_{\eta_{3}s_{3},\eta_{4}s_{4}}\delta_{\eta_{3},\eta_{4}}\delta_{s_{3},s_{4}}\sum_{a_{3},a_{4}\in\{3.4\}}[\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}}g_{c}(i\omega)+(1-\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}})g_{c,off}(i\omega)]

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\delta_{a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}\delta_{a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}\delta_{a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(1-\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}})

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}[\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{3}}\delta_{a_{1},a_{3}}+\delta_{\eta_{1},-\eta_{3}}(1-\delta_{a_{1},a_{3}})]\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\delta_{a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3},a_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(1-\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}})\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{2}(\delta_{a_{1},3}+\delta_{a_{1},4})(\delta_{a_{2},3}+\delta_{a_{2},4})\sum_{a_{3},a_{4}\in\{3.4\}}[\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}}g_{c}(i\omega)+(1-\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}})g_{c,off}(i\omega)]

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\delta_{a_{2},a_{3}}\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}}+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{3}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\delta_{a_{2},a_{4}}(1-\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}})

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}[\delta_{a_{1},a_{3}}\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}}(2-\delta_{a_{1},a_{3}})+2(1-\delta_{a_{1},a_{3}})\delta_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}}]\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{2}\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{2}}\delta_{s_{1},s_{2}}(\delta_{a_{1},3}+\delta_{a_{1},4})(\delta_{a_{2},3}+\delta_{a_{2},4})\sum_{a_{3},a_{4}\in\{3.4\}}[\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}}g_{c}(i\omega)+(1-\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}})g_{c,off}(i\omega)]

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}\delta_{a_{1},a_{2}}\delta_{a_{2},a_{3}}\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}}+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\delta_{a_{1},a_{3}}\delta_{a_{2},a_{4}}(1-\delta_{a_{1},a_{2}})+\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}(2-\delta_{a_{1},a_{3}})\delta_{a_{1},a_{2}}\delta_{a_{3},a_{4}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{2}\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{2}}\delta_{s_{1},s_{2}}(\delta_{a_{1},3}+\delta_{a_{1},4})(\delta_{a_{2},3}+\delta_{a_{2},4})

\displaystyle\bigg[g_{c}(i\omega)\delta_{a_{1},a_{2}}\bigg(\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}}+3\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})}{N_{f}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg)+g_{c,off}(i\omega)(1-\delta_{a_{1},a_{2}})\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{2}\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{2}}\delta_{s_{1},s_{2}}(\delta_{a_{1},3}+\delta_{a_{1},4})(\delta_{a_{2},3}+\delta_{a_{2},4})\bigg[g_{c}(i\omega)\delta_{a_{1},a_{2}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}+g_{c,off}(i\omega)(1-\delta_{a_{1},a_{2}})\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]

(S384)

To be specific, we have

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{3\eta_{1}s_{1},3\eta_{2}s_{2}}=[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{4\eta_{1}s_{1},4\eta_{2}s_{2}}=\delta_{\eta_{1}s_{1},\eta_{2}s_{2}}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}J^{2}g_{c}(i\omega)=\delta_{\eta_{1}s_{1},\eta_{2}s_{2}}\frac{31J^{2}}{28}g_{c}(i\omega)

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{3\eta_{1}s_{1},4\eta_{2}s_{2}}=[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{4\eta_{1}s_{1},3\eta_{2}s_{2}}=\delta_{\eta_{1}s_{1},\eta_{2}s_{2}}\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}J^{2}g_{c,off}(i\omega)=-\delta_{\eta_{1}s_{1},\eta_{2}s_{2}}\frac{J^{2}}{28}g_{c,off}(i\omega)

(S385)

where we take
N_{f}=8
and
n_{f}=N_{f}/2=4
in the final expression.
Denote the diagonal and off-diagonal
c
electron self-energy as

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{3\eta_{1}s_{1},3\eta_{1}s_{1}}=[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{4\eta_{1}s_{1},4\eta_{1}s_{1}}=\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}J^{2}g_{c}(i\omega)

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{3\eta_{1}s_{1},4\eta_{1}s_{1}}=[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{4\eta_{1}s_{1},3\eta_{1}s_{1}}=\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}J^{2}g_{c,off}(i\omega)

(S386)

Then the
c
electron self-energy could be written as

[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{a_{1}\eta_{1},s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}=\delta_{\eta_{1}s_{1},\eta_{2}s_{2}}\begin{pmatrix}0_{2\times 2}&0_{2\times 2}\\
0_{2\times 2}&\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)\sigma_{0}+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)\sigma_{x}\end{pmatrix}_{a_{1},a_{2}}

(S387)

To calculate
g_{c}(i\omega)
and
g_{c,off}(i\omega)
, we introduce the spectral function

\displaystyle\rho_{c}(\epsilon)=\frac{1}{\pi}\mathrm{Im}[g_{c}(\epsilon-i0^{+})]

\displaystyle\rho_{c,off}(\epsilon)=\frac{1}{\pi}\mathrm{Im}[g_{c,off}(\epsilon-i0^{+})]

(S388)

which are expressed as

\displaystyle\rho_{c}(\epsilon)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Omega_{M}}\int_{|{\mathbf{k}}|<\Lambda_{c}}\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\bigg(1+\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)[\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1})+\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4})]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)[\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2})+\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3})]\bigg]d^{2}k

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\pi}{\Omega_{M}}\int_{0}^{\Lambda_{c}}\frac{1}{4}\bigg[\bigg(1+\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}k|^{2}}}\bigg)\delta(|\epsilon|-\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2})

\displaystyle+\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}k|^{2}}}\bigg)\delta(|\epsilon|-\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2})\bigg]kdk

\displaystyle\rho_{c,off}(\epsilon)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Omega_{M}}\int_{|{\mathbf{k}}|<\Lambda_{c}}\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\bigg(1+\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)[-\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1})+\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4})]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg)[\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2})-\delta(\epsilon-E^{\eta,c}_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3})]\bigg]d^{2}k

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\pi}{\Omega_{M}}\int_{0}^{\Lambda_{c}}\frac{1}{4}\bigg[\bigg(1+\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}k|^{2}}}\bigg)\delta(|\epsilon|-\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2})\mathrm{sgn}(\epsilon)

\displaystyle-\bigg(1-\frac{M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}k|^{2}}}\bigg)\delta(|\epsilon|-\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}k|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2})\mathrm{sgn}(\epsilon)\bigg]kdk

(S389)

Using the integration strategy in
eq.

S256
, the spectrum functions are further calculated as

\displaystyle\rho_{c}(\epsilon)=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\bigg[|\epsilon|\theta(|\epsilon|-M)\theta(\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}-|\epsilon|)+|\epsilon|\theta(|\epsilon|+M)\theta(\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}-|\epsilon|)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}|\epsilon|[\theta(|\epsilon|-M)\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)+\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}|\epsilon|\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)-\frac{\pi}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}|\epsilon|\theta(M-|\epsilon|)

\displaystyle\rho_{c,off}(\epsilon)=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\mathrm{sgn}(\epsilon)\bigg[|\epsilon|\theta(|\epsilon|-M)\theta(\frac{M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}-|\epsilon|)-|\epsilon|\theta(|\epsilon|+M)\theta(\frac{-M+\sqrt{4|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|^{2}+M^{2}}}{2}-|\epsilon|)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\epsilon[\theta(|\epsilon|-M)\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)-\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\pi}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\epsilon\theta(M-|\epsilon|)

(S390)

where we have used
|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|\gg M
so that
\theta(M-|\epsilon|)\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\epsilon|)=\theta(M-|\epsilon|)
.
Then similar to
section

IV.4
we obtain

\displaystyle g_{c}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle\int_{\epsilon}\rho_{c}(\epsilon)\frac{1}{i\omega-\epsilon}=-i\omega\frac{\pi}{|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)+{i\omega\frac{\pi}{2|v_{\star}^{2}|\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg( \frac{M^2 +\omega^2}{\omega^2}
\bigg)}

\displaystyle g_{c,off}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle\int_{\epsilon}\rho_{c,off}(\epsilon)\frac{1}{i\omega-\epsilon}=\frac{\pi M}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}-i\omega\frac{\pi}{2|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\log\bigg(\frac{i\omega+M}{i\omega-M}\bigg)

(S391)

Using the same analytic continuation method as in
section

IV.4
, the real-frequency Green’s function reads

\displaystyle g_{c}(\omega+i0^{+})=-\frac{\pi}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\omega\bigg[\log\bigg(
\bigg|
\frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2 -\omega^2 }
{\omega^2}
\bigg|
\bigg)-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(
\bigg|
\frac{M^2 -\omega^2 }
{\omega^2}
\bigg|
\bigg)\bigg]-i\pi\rho_{c}(\omega)

\displaystyle g_{c,off}(\omega+i0^{+})=\frac{\pi}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}\bigg[M+\frac{1}{2}\omega\log\bigg(\bigg|
\frac{\omega-M}{\omega+M}\bigg|\bigg)\bigg]-i\pi\rho_{c,off}(\omega)

(S392)

Note that in the hybridization function calculation
section

IV.4
, we are evaluating the
c
-electron Greens’s function in
\Gamma_{3}
sector. Here we are computing in the
\Gamma_{1}\oplus\Gamma_{2}
sector.

VII.2.1
M=0

We first discuss the self-energy of
c
electron in the
M=0
limit.
For
M=0
, we find

\displaystyle g_{c}(i\omega)=-\frac{\pi}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}i\omega\log\bigg(
\frac{(i\omega)^2 -|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{(i\omega)^2}
\bigg),\quad g_{c,off}(i\omega)=0

(S393)

The
c
electron self-energy then reads (from
eq.

S386
)

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi i\omega\log\bigg(
\frac{(i\omega)^2 -|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{(i\omega)^2}
\bigg)

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle 0

(S394)

where we have introduced the dimensionless parameter (
eq.

S23
)

\displaystyle j=\frac{J^{2}}{|v_{\star}|^{2}\Omega_{M}}

(S395)

In real frequency we have

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(\omega+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg[\omega\log\bigg(\bigg|
\frac{\omega^2 -|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|
\bigg)+i\pi|\omega|\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\omega|)\bigg]

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(\omega+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle 0

(S396)

At
M=0
, the non-interacting Green’s function of
c
electron reads (
eq.

S364
)

\displaystyle[G_{c}^{S_{decouple}}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1}_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}=\delta_{s_{1},s_{2}}\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{2}}\bigg[i\omega\mathbb{I}-\begin{pmatrix}0_{2\times 2}&v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}+ik_{y}\sigma_{z})\\
v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}-ik_{y}\sigma_{z})&0_{2\times 2}\end{pmatrix}\bigg]_{a_{1},a_{2}}

(S397)

The interacting Green’s function follows (
eqs.

S365
and
S387
)

\displaystyle[G_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]^{-1}_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}=\delta_{s_{1},s_{2}}\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{2}}\bigg[i\omega\mathbb{I}-\begin{pmatrix}0_{2\times 2}&v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}+ik_{y}\sigma_{z})\\
v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}-ik_{y}\sigma_{z})&\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)\sigma_{0}\end{pmatrix}\bigg]_{a_{1},a_{2}}

(S398)

The trace of the Green’s function now reads

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}[G_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]=

\displaystyle\frac{8[2i\omega-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]}{(i\omega)^{2}-i\omega\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{8[2i\omega-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]}{(i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{j}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1})(i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{j}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{8[2i\omega-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]}{\mathcal{E}^{j}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}-\mathcal{E}^{j}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{j}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{j}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}}\bigg)

(S399)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}=

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)/2-\sqrt{[\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}=-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|+\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)/2+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}=

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)/2+\sqrt{[\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]^{2}/4+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}=|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|+\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)/2+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

(S400)

At
j=0
and
\Sigma_{c}(i\omega)=0
, the bare dispersions are

E^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|,\quad E^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|

(S401)

In real frequency, we write

\mathcal{E}^{j}_{\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}},n}=\mathcal{R}^{j}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}-i\Gamma^{j}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}

(S402)

Then the renormalized dispersions
\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
and scattering times
\tau^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
are determined by

\displaystyle(\omega-\mathcal{R}^{j}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}=0,\quad\frac{1}{\tau^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}=\Gamma^{j}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}

(S403)

Then up to first order of
j
we have

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}=\mathcal{R}^{j}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega=E^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}},\quad\frac{1}{\tau^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}=\Gamma^{j}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega=E^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}

(S404)

To be specific,

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=-\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=

\displaystyle-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|+\mathrm{Re}[\Sigma_{c,dia}(-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|+i0^{+})]/2

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)\pi j}{2N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\log\bigg(\bigg|
\frac{|v_{\star}\kk|^2 -|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{|v_{\star}\kk|^2}\bigg|
\bigg)+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)\pi j}{2N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\log\bigg(
\frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{|v_{\star}\kk|^2}
\bigg)+\mathcal{O}(j^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}=\frac{1}{\tau^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}}=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{Im}[\Sigma_{c,dia}(-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|+i0^{+})]/2

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)\pi^{2}j}{2N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

(S405)

At charge neutrality with
N_{f}=8
and
n_{f}=4
, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=-\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}\approx

\displaystyle-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|\frac{31\pi j}{56}\log\bigg(
\frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{|v_{\star}\kk|^2}
\bigg)+\mathcal{O}(j^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}=\frac{1}{\tau^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}}=

\displaystyle|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|\frac{31\pi^{2}j}{56}+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

(S406)

Taking the parameters of the topological heavy-fermion model in Ref.
174
, we find
j\approx 0.006
, corresponding to
\frac{31\pi^{2}j}{56}\approx 0.03
, which leads to an almost vanishing damping rate compared to the bare dispersion
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|
.
As a comparison, the damping rate of the gapless modes induced by the single-particle Hybridization
\gamma
is approximately
0.18|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|
.

VII.2.2
Finite
M

We next consider the effect of a finite
M
.
The
c
electron self-energy is evaluated as

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi i\omega\bigg[\log\bigg(
\frac{(i\omega)^2 -|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{(i\omega)^2}
\bigg)-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\frac{(i\omega)^2-M^2}{(i\omega)^2}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)=

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg[M+\frac{i\omega}{2}\log\bigg(\frac{i\omega-M}{i\omega+M}\bigg)\bigg]

(S407)

which in real frequency becomes

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(\omega+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\omega\bigg[\log\bigg(\bigg|
\frac{\omega^2 -|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|
\bigg)-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2-\omega^2}{\omega^2}\bigg|\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}|\omega|\bigg[\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|\omega|)-\frac{1}{2}\theta(M-|\omega|)\bigg]

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(\omega+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg[M+\frac{\omega}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{\omega-M}{\omega+M}\bigg|\bigg)\bigg]+j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}\omega\theta(M-|\omega|)

(S408)

Besides, the interacting Green’s function is written as

\displaystyle[G_{c}^{-1}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{a_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}=\delta_{s_{1},s_{2}}\delta_{\eta_{1},\eta_{2}}\bigg[i\omega\mathbb{I}-\begin{pmatrix}0_{2\times 2}&v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}+ik_{y}\sigma_{z})\\
v_{\star}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{0}-ik_{y}\sigma_{z})&\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)\sigma_{0}+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)\sigma_{x}+M\sigma_{x}\end{pmatrix}\bigg]_{a_{1},a_{2}}

(S409)

The trace of the Green’s function reads

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}[G_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]=

\displaystyle\frac{4[2i\omega-M-\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]}{(i\omega)^{2}-i\omega[M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)+\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}+\frac{4[2i\omega+M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]}{(i\omega)^{2}+i\omega[M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4[2i\omega-M-\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]}{\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}-\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}}-\frac{1}{i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{4[2i\omega+M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]}{\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},3}-\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},4}}\bigg(\frac{1}{i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},3}}-\frac{1}{i\omega-\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},4}}\bigg)

(S410)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}=

\displaystyle\frac{M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)+\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)}{2}-\sqrt{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+[M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)+\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]^{2}/4}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}=

\displaystyle\frac{M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)+\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)}{2}+\sqrt{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+[M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)+\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]^{2}/4}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},3}=

\displaystyle-\frac{M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)}{2}-\sqrt{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+[M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]^{2}/4}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},4}=

\displaystyle-\frac{M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)}{2}+\sqrt{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+[M+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)]^{2}/4}

(S411)

Without the dynamical self-energy
j=0
, we recover the bare dispersions
E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}=\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{j=0}
with

\displaystyle E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=

\displaystyle-E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},4}=M/2-\sqrt{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}/4}

\displaystyle E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=

\displaystyle-E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},3}=M/2+\sqrt{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+M^{2}/4}

(S412)

Then expand up to first order of
j
we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},1}=

\displaystyle E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\bigg[1-\frac{\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},2}=

\displaystyle E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}\bigg[1+\frac{\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)+\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},3}=

\displaystyle E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},3}\bigg[1-\frac{\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{i\omega,{\mathbf{k}},4}=

\displaystyle E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},4}\bigg[1+\frac{\Sigma_{c,dia}(i\omega)-\Sigma_{c,off}(i\omega)}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

In real frequency, denote

\mathcal{E}^{j,M}_{\omega+i0^{+},{\mathbf{k}},n}=\mathcal{R}^{j,M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}-i\Gamma^{j,M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}

(S414)

Then the renormalized dispersions
\tilde{E}^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
and scattering times
\tau^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}
are determined by

\displaystyle(\omega-\mathcal{R}^{j}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n})\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}=0,\quad\frac{1}{\tau^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}=\Gamma^{j}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega=\tilde{E}^{j}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}

(S415)

which, up to first order of
j
are

\tilde{E}^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}=\mathcal{R}^{j,M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega=E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}+\mathcal{O}(j^{2}),\quad\frac{1}{\tau^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}=\Gamma^{j,M}_{\omega,{\mathbf{k}},n}\bigg|_{\omega=E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}}+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

(S416)

To get the final expression, we need to evaluate
\Sigma_{c}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},n}+i0^{+})
.
Here we focus on small
|{\mathbf{k}}|
.
Up to the lowest order of
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|
, the bare dispersions are

\displaystyle E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=-E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},4}\approx

\displaystyle-\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

\displaystyle E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=-E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},3}\approx

\displaystyle M+\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

(S417)

Then the self-energies are (
eq.

S408
)

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg(-\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}\bigg)\bigg[\log\bigg(\bigg|
\frac{|v_{\star}\kk|^4 -M^2|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{|v_{\star}\kk|^4}\bigg|
\bigg)-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^4-|v_{\star}\kk|^4}{|v_{\star}\kk|^4}\bigg|\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{2N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}\log\bigg(
\frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{|v_{\star}\kk|^2}
\bigg)-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{2N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg[M-\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{2M}\log\bigg(\bigg|\frac{M^2+|v_{\star}\kk|^2}{M^2-|v_{\star}\kk|^2}\bigg|\bigg)\bigg]-j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi M-j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi M\bigg[\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-M^2}{M^2}
\bigg)-\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\frac{2|v_{\star}\kk|^2}{M^2}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}M+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi M\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-M^2}{\sqrt{2}|v_{\star}\kk|M}
\bigg)-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}M+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg[M+\frac{M}{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\kk|^2}{2M^2}\bigg)\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},3}+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(-E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})=-[\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})]^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi M\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-M^2}{\sqrt{2}|v_{\star}\kk|M}
\bigg)-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}M+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},3}+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(-E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})=[\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})]^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg[M+\frac{M}{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\kk|^2}{2M^2}\bigg)\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},4}+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,dia}(-E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})=-[\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})]^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}\log\bigg(
\frac{|v_\star\Lambda_c|^2}{|v_{\star}\kk|^2}
\bigg)-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{2N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},4}+i0^{+})=

\displaystyle\Sigma_{c,off}(-E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})=[\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})]^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi M+j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}i\pi^{2}\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

Finally, the renormalized dispersions and scattering times are obtained as

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}=

\displaystyle-\tilde{E}^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},4}=E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\bigg[1-\frac{\mathrm{Re}[\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})+\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})]}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\bigg[1-\frac{j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi M}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}\bigg[1-j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},4}}=E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}\frac{\mathrm{Im}[\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})+\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},1}+i0^{+})]}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4}}{M^{2}}\frac{\pi^{2}j}{M}\bigg[\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{2N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}+\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{6})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4}}{M^{2}}\frac{\pi^{2}j}{M}\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-3)}{2N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}+\mathcal{O}(j^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{6})

\displaystyle\tilde{E}^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}=

\displaystyle-\tilde{E}^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},3}=E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}\bigg[1+\frac{\mathrm{Re}[\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})+\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})]}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg(M+\frac{|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}{M}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4})\bigg)\bigg[1-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-M^2}{\sqrt{2}|v_{\star}\kk|M}
\bigg)

\displaystyle+j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg[1+\frac{1}{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\kk|^2}{2M^2}\bigg)\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle M\bigg[1-j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\Lambda_c|^2-M^2}{\sqrt{2}|v_{\star}\kk|M}
\bigg)+j\frac{n_{f}(n_{f}-N_{f})}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi\bigg[1+\log\bigg(\frac{|v_{\star}\kk|}{\sqrt{2}M}\bigg)\bigg]\bigg]+\mathcal{O}(j^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},3}}=-E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}\frac{\mathrm{Im}[\Sigma_{c,dia}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})+\Sigma_{c,off}(E^{j,M}_{{\mathbf{k}},2}+i0^{+})]}{\sqrt{M^{2}+4|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j\frac{n_{f}(N_{f}-n_{f})(4N_{f}-1)}{N_{f}^{2}(N_{f}-1)}\pi^{2}M+\mathcal{O}(j^{2},|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2})

(S419)

For the gapless modes
n=1,4
, we observe that at first order of
j
, the self-energy introduces a weak damping proportional to
|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{4}
.
Physically, the self-energy correction arising from the
J
term acts on the
\Gamma_{1}\oplus\Gamma_{2}
electrons, which are already gapped by the
M
term. As a result, the gapless mode formed by the
\Gamma_{3}
electrons does not experience a strong damping effect near
\Gamma
point.
As a comparison, the gapped modes
n=2,3
formed by
\Gamma_{1}\oplus\Gamma_{2}
electrons do have a finite damping rate at
\Gamma
point.
At charge neutrality, the damping rate reads

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau_{{\mathbf{k}},n=1}^{j,M}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau_{{\mathbf{k}},n=4}^{j,M}}=\bigg[0+\mathcal{O}(j^{2})\bigg]|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{2}+\mathcal{O}(|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|^{3})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau_{{\mathbf{k}},n=2}^{j,M}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau_{{\mathbf{k}},n=3}^{j,M}}=M\frac{31\pi^{2}j}{56}

(S420)

Taking the parameters of the topological heavy-fermion model in Ref.
174
, we find the finite scattering rate of the gapped modes is
0.03M\approx 0.012
meV.

VII.3
Effect of strain

In
eqs.

S380
and
S381
, we proved that at low temperature, only the
\Gamma_{1}\oplus\Gamma_{2}

c
electrons acquire a dynamical self-energy at order
j\sim J^{2}
due to the local-moment fluctuations of
f
electrons.
Thus, we expect that if the local-momentum fluctuations are quenched, the damping rate will also be suppressed at order
j
.
To verify this, we investigate the effect of finite strain, which will frozen the local-momentum fluctuations at charge neutrality in the low-temperature limit.
For simplicity, we focus on the case of
M=0
.

The strained Hamiltonian of the
f
sector takes the form of

\displaystyle H_{strain,f}=\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\alpha^{\prime},\eta s}[M_{f}(\varepsilon_{xy}\sigma_{x}+\varepsilon_{-}\eta\sigma_{y})_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}-\mu]f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha^{\prime}\eta s}

(S421)

We can perform a rotation characterized by

\displaystyle U_{\alpha\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{\eta}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{\varepsilon_{xy}-i\eta\varepsilon_{-}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon_{xy}^{2}+\varepsilon_{-}^{2}}}&-\frac{\varepsilon_{xy}-i\eta\varepsilon_{-}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon_{xy}^{2}+\varepsilon_{-}^{2}}}\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}_{\alpha\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}

(S422)

and let

\displaystyle f_{{\mathbf{R}},\alpha\eta s}=\sum_{\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}U^{\eta}_{\alpha\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}\eta s}

(S423)

where we use
\tilde{\alpha}=1,2
to label the new orbital basis and use
\tilde{f}
to denote the corresponding annihilation operators.
In the new electron basis
\tilde{f}
, the Hamiltonian reads

\displaystyle H_{strain,f}=

\displaystyle\sum_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}\eta s}[\varepsilon(-1)^{\tilde{\alpha}+1}-\mu]\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}\eta s}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}\eta s}

(S424)

where

\displaystyle\varepsilon=M_{f}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{xy}^{2}+\varepsilon_{-}^{2}}

(S425)

The atomic Hamiltonian of
f
electrons at charge neutrality (
\mu=0
) now becomes

\displaystyle H_{atom,strain}=

\displaystyle\sum_{{\mathbf{R}}}H_{{\mathbf{R}},strain}

\displaystyle H_{{\mathbf{R}},strain}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\tilde{\alpha}\eta s}\varepsilon(-1)^{\tilde{\alpha}+1}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}\eta s}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}\eta s}+\frac{U_{1}}{2}\bigg(\sum_{\tilde{\alpha}\eta s}(\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}\eta s}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}\eta s}-1/2)\bigg)^{2}

(S426)

For a given site, the eigenstate of
H_{{\mathbf{R}},strain}
can be characterized by the filling of
\tilde{f}
electrons of each orbital
\tilde{\alpha}
. We let

\displaystyle|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle

(S427)

denote the state with

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta s}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}\eta s}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}\eta s}|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle=n_{\tilde{\alpha}}|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle

(S428)

and
i=1,...,d_{n_{1},n_{2}}
labels the degeneracy of the states with the same filling factor
n_{1},n_{2}
and

\displaystyle d_{n_{1},n_{2}}=\binom{4}{n_{1}}\binom{4}{n_{2}}

(S429)

The energy of
|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle
state is

\displaystyle H_{{\mathbf{R}},strain}|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle=E_{n_{1},n_{2}}|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle

\displaystyle E_{n_{1},n_{2}}=\varepsilon(n_{1}-n_{2})+\frac{U_{1}}{2}(n_{1}+n_{2}-4)^{2}

(S430)

Then the partition function is

Z_{{\mathbf{R}},strain}=\sum_{n_{1},n_{2}}d_{n_{1},n_{2}}e^{-\beta E_{n_{1},n_{2}}}

(S431)

At charge neutrality, the ground state
|n_{1}=0,n_{2}=4,i=1\rangle
is singly degenerate
d_{0,4}=1
, with ground state energy

E_{gs,strain}=E_{n_{1}=0,n_{2}=4}=-4\varepsilon

(S432)

Thus in the low-temperature limit, we have

\lim_{\beta\to+\infty}Z_{{\mathbf{R}},strain}=e^{-\beta E_{gs,strain}}=e^{-\beta E_{0,4}}

(S433)

We now investigate the
\tilde{f}
electron correlation function

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathbf{R}},strain}}\sum_{n_{1},n_{2},m_{1},m_{2},i,j}\langle n_{1},n_{2},i|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}|m_{1},m_{2},j\rangle\langle m_{1},m_{2},j|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle

\displaystyle\begin{cases}e^{-(\beta-\tau_{1}+\tau_{2})E_{n_{1},n_{2}}-(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})E_{m_{1},m_{2}}}&\tau_{1}>\tau_{2}\\
e^{-(\beta-\tau_{2}+\tau_{1})E_{m_{1},m_{2}}-(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})E_{n_{1},n_{2}}}&\tau_{1}<\tau_{2}\\
\end{cases}

(S434)

In the Matsubara frequency domain, we find

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}\langle T_{\tau}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}e^{i\Omega(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathbf{R}},strain}}\sum_{n_{1},n_{2},m_{1},m_{2},i,j}\langle n_{1},n_{2},i|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}|m_{1},m_{2},j\rangle\langle m_{1},m_{2},j|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle\chi(i\Omega,E_{m_{1},m_{2}},E_{n_{1},n_{2}})

(S435)

where

\displaystyle\chi(i\Omega,E_{m_{1},m_{2}},E_{n_{1},n_{2}})=(1-\delta_{E_{m_{1},m_{2}},E_{n_{1},n_{2}}})\frac{e^{-\beta E_{m_{1},m_{2}}}-e^{-\beta E_{n_{1},n_{2}}}}{i\Omega+E_{n_{1},n_{2}}-E_{m_{1},m_{2}}}+\delta_{E_{m_{1},m_{2}},E_{n_{1},n_{2}}}\beta e^{-\beta E_{n_{1},n_{2}}}

(S436)

Using
eq.

S433
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lim_{\beta\to+\infty}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathbf{R}},strain}}\chi(i\Omega,E_{m_{1},m_{2}},E_{n_{1},n_{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\delta_{(m_{1},m_{2}),(n_{1}n_{2})})\frac{\delta_{(m_{1},m_{2}),(0,4)}-\delta_{(n_{1},n_{2}),(0,4)}}{i\Omega+E_{n_{1},n_{2}}-E_{m_{1},m_{2}}}+\beta\delta_{(m_{1},m_{2}),(n_{1},n_{2})}\delta_{(m_{1},m_{2}),(0,4)}

(S437)

Thus in the low-temperature limit, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\beta\to+\infty}\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}\langle T_{\tau}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}e^{i\Omega(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{(n_{1},n_{2})\neq(0,4),i}\langle n_{1},n_{2},i|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}|0,4,1\rangle\langle 0,4,1|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle\frac{1}{i\Omega+E_{n_{1},n_{2}}-E_{0,4}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{(m_{1},m_{2})\neq(0,4),j}\langle 0,4,1|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}|m_{1},m_{2},j\rangle\langle m_{1},m_{2},j|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}|0,4,1\rangle\frac{1}{i\Omega+E_{0,4}-E_{m_{1},m_{2}}}

\displaystyle+\beta\langle 0,4,1|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}|0,4,1\rangle\langle 0,4,1|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}|0,4,1\rangle

(S438)

The matrix element is evaluated as

\displaystyle\langle n_{1},n_{2},i|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}|0,4,1\rangle=\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},2}\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{2},\eta_{2},s_{2})}\delta_{(n_{1},n_{2}),(0,4)}+\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{1},1}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},2}\delta_{\ket{n_1,n_2,i},\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\ket{0,4,1}}

(S439)

Thus, for
(n_{1},n_{2})\neq(0,4)
, we have

\displaystyle\langle n_{1},n_{2},i|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}|0,4,1\rangle\langle 0,4,1|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}|n_{1},n_{2},i\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{1},1}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},2}\delta_{\ket{n_1,n_2,i},\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\ket{0,4,1}}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{4},1}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{3},2}\delta_{\ket{n_1,n_2,i},\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}\ket{0,4,1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{4},\eta_{4},s_{4})}\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{2},\eta_{2},s_{2}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{3},\eta_{3},s_{3})}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{1},1}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},2}\delta_{(n_{1},n_{2}),(1,3)}\delta_{\ket{n_1,n_2,i},\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\ket{0,4,1}}

(S440)

Similarly, for
