(m_{1},m_{2})\neq(0,4)
we have

\displaystyle\langle 0,4,1|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}|m_{1},m_{2},j\rangle\langle m_{1},m_{2},j|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}|0,4,1\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{4},\eta_{4},s_{4})}\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{2},\eta_{2},s_{2}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{3},\eta_{3},s_{3})}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{1},2}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},1}\delta_{(n_{1},n_{2}),(1,3)}\delta_{\ket{m_1,m_2,j},\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}\ket{0,4,1}}

(S441)

In addition,

\displaystyle\langle 0,4,1|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}|0,4,1\rangle\langle 0,4,1|\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}|0,4,1\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},2}\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{2},\eta_{2},s_{2})}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{3},2}\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{3},\eta_{3},s_{3}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{4},\eta_{4},s_{4})}

(S442)

Accumulating all contributions, we find

\displaystyle\lim_{\beta\to+\infty}\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}\langle T_{\tau}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}e^{i\Omega(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{2},\eta_{2},s_{2})}\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{3},\eta_{3},s_{3}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{4},\eta_{4},s_{4})}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},2}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{3},2}\beta

\displaystyle+\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{4},\eta_{4},s_{4})}\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{2},\eta_{2},s_{2}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{3},\eta_{3},s_{3})}(\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{1},2}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},1}\frac{-1}{i\Omega-2\varepsilon}+\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{1},1}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},2}\frac{1}{i\Omega+2\varepsilon})

(S443)

where we have used
E_{1,3}-E_{0,4}=2\varepsilon
.
Define the normal order
:O:=O-\langle O\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}
.
The expectation value of two Fermion operator is

\displaystyle\langle\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}=\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{2},\eta_{2},s_{2})}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{1},2}

(S444)

Therefore

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}:\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{1})::\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{1})\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}\langle\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(\tau_{2})\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}-\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{2},\eta_{2},s_{2})}\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{3},\eta_{3},s_{3}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{4},\eta_{4},s_{4})}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},2}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{3},2}

(S445)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}\langle T_{\tau}:\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{1})::\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})\tilde{f}_{{\mathbf{R}},\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{H_{atom,strain}}e^{i\Omega(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{4},\eta_{4},s_{4})}\delta_{(\tilde{\alpha}_{2},\eta_{2},s_{2}),(\tilde{\alpha}_{3},\eta_{3},s_{3})}(\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{1},2}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},1}\frac{-1}{i\Omega-2\varepsilon}+\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{1},1}\delta_{\tilde{\alpha}_{2},2}\frac{1}{i\Omega+2\varepsilon})

(S446)

In the original
f
-electron basis (using
eq.

S422
), we find

\displaystyle\chi_{\alpha_{1}\eta_{1}s_{1},\alpha_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2},\alpha_{3}\eta_{3}s_{3},\alpha_{4}\eta_{4}s_{4}}(i\Omega)=\sum_{\tilde{\alpha}}\delta_{(\eta_{1},s_{1}),(\eta_{4},s_{4})}\delta_{(\eta_{2},s_{2}),(\eta_{3},s_{3})}U^{\eta_{1},*}_{\alpha_{1},\tilde{\alpha}}U^{\eta_{2}}_{\alpha_{2},3-\tilde{\alpha}}U^{\eta_{2},*}_{\alpha_{3},3-\tilde{\alpha}}U^{\eta_{1}}_{\alpha_{4},\tilde{\alpha}}\frac{1}{2\varepsilon+(-1)^{\tilde{\alpha}+1}i\Omega}

(S447)

We then utilize
eqs.

S367
,
S393
and
S447
and obtain

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\approx-\delta_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\beta}\sum_{i\Omega}g_{c}(i\omega-i\Omega)J^{2}\frac{4\varepsilon}{(2\varepsilon)^{2}-(i\Omega)^{2}}

(S448)

where, for simplicity, we have dropped the effect of strain on the
c
-electron.
We utilize the following spectral representation of Green’s function

\displaystyle g_{c}(i\omega)=\int_{E}\frac{\rho_{c}(E)}{i\omega-E},\quad\rho_{c}(E)=\frac{1}{2\Omega_{M}}\int_{|{\mathbf{k}}|<\Lambda_{c}}\bigg[\delta(E-|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|)+\delta(E+|v_{\star}{\mathbf{k}}|)\bigg]=\frac{\pi|E|}{\Omega_{M}|v_{\star}|^{2}}\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}|-|E|)

(S449)

We find

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\approx-\delta_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\beta}\sum_{i\Omega}J^{2}\int_{E}\frac{\rho_{c}(E)}{i\omega-i\Omega-E}\bigg[\frac{1}{i\Omega+2\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{-i\Omega+2\varepsilon}\bigg]

(S450)

We then use (in the
\beta\rightarrow\infty
limit)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\sum_{i\Omega}\frac{1}{i\omega-i\Omega-E}\bigg[\frac{1}{i\Omega+2\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{-i\Omega+2\varepsilon}\bigg]\approx\frac{\theta(E)}{i\omega-2\varepsilon-E}+\frac{\theta(-E)}{i\omega+2\varepsilon-E}

(S451)

Combining
eqs.

S451
and
S450
, we find

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}(i\omega,{\mathbf{k}})]_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\approx

\displaystyle-\delta_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}J^{2}\int_{E}\rho_{c}(E)\bigg[\frac{\theta(E)}{i\omega-2\varepsilon-E}+\frac{\theta(-E)}{i\omega+2\varepsilon-E}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\delta_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\frac{\pi J^{2}}{\Omega_{M}|v_{\star}|^{2}}\bigg[-(i\omega-2\varepsilon)\log\bigg(
\frac{i\omega-2\varepsilon-|v_\star\Lambda_c|}{i\omega-2\varepsilon}
\bigg)-(i\omega+2\varepsilon)\log\bigg(
\frac{i\omega+2\varepsilon+|v_\star\Lambda_c|}{i\omega+2\varepsilon}
\bigg)\bigg]

(S452)

In the real-frequency, we find

\displaystyle[\Sigma_{c}({\mathbf{k}},\omega-i0^{+})]_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\approx

\displaystyle\delta_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\frac{\pi J^{2}}{\Omega_{M}|v_{\star}|^{2}}\bigg[(\omega-2\varepsilon)\log\bigg( \bigg|
\frac{\omega-2\varepsilon-|v_\star\Lambda_c|}{\omega-2\varepsilon}\bigg|
\bigg)+(\omega+2\varepsilon)\log\bigg( \bigg|
\frac{\omega+2\varepsilon+|v_\star\Lambda_c|}{\omega+2\varepsilon}
\bigg)\bigg|\bigg]

\displaystyle+i\delta_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\pi\frac{\pi J^{2}}{\Omega_{M}|v_{\star}|^{2}}\bigg(|\omega|-2\varepsilon\bigg)\theta(|\omega|-2\varepsilon)\theta(|v_{\star}\Lambda_{c}+2\varepsilon|-|\omega|)

(S453)

We can observe that, the scattering rate of the
c
electrons (
\mathrm{Im}[\Sigma_{c}({\mathbf{k}},\omega-i0^{+})]
) vanishes at low-energy limit with
|\omega|<2\varepsilon
.
When a finite strain is applied, the fluctuations of the

f
electrons are quenched at low temperatures, because the
f
-electron ground state becomes non-degenerate in the atomic limit.
In the absence of low-energy
f
-electron
fluctuations, the scattering rate is correspondingly suppressed and
approaches zero.
